> AGENT

`agents/` ディレクトリにあります。`data-agent` という名前の Cloudflare Workers 上で動作する AI エージェントです。

主な構成:
- **`agents/src/agent.ts`** - エージェント本体（Durable Object `DataAgent`）
- **`agents/src/tools.ts`** - エージェントが使えるツール定義
- **`agents/src/slack.ts`** - Slack連携
- **`agents/src/index.ts`** - エントリポイント
- **`agents/wrangler.jsonc`** - Wrangler設定（DO + SQLite使用）

詳しく中身を見ますか？

> DEVELOPER

これをCloudflare Agents, AI Gateway, Vectorize, Workers AI, AI Search, D1, R2などを存分に使ったアーキテクチャにしてほしい。

1	import { AIChatAgent } from "@cloudflare/ai-chat";
2	import { convertToModelMessages, stepCountIs, streamText } from "ai";
3	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
4	import { createTools, SYSTEM_PROMPT } from "./tools";
5	import type { Env } from "./types";
6	
7	const MODEL_ID = "@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast" as const;
8	
9	export class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {
10	  async onChatMessage(
11	    _onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>["onChatMessage"]>[0],
12	    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>["onChatMessage"]>[1]
13	  ) {
14	    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });
15	
16	    const result = streamText({
17	      model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
18	      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
19	      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),
20	      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,
21	      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
22	      tools: createTools(this.env.DB),
23	    });
24	
25	    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
26	  }
27	}
28	
```

1	import { routeAgentRequest } from "agents";
2	import { handleSlackEvent } from "./slack";
3	import type { Env } from "./types";
4	
5	export { DataAgent } from "./agent";
6	
7	export default {
8	  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
9	    const url = new URL(request.url);
10	
11	    if (url.pathname === "/health") {
12	      return new Response("OK");
13	    }
14	
15	    // Slack Events API
16	    if (url.pathname === "/slack/events" && request.method === "POST") {
17	      // Pass waitUntil so Slack handler can process AI response in background
18	      globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL = (p: Promise<unknown>) => ctx.waitUntil(p);
19	      return handleSlackEvent(request, env);
20	    }
21	
22	    // Agent WebSocket routing
23	    const agentResponse = await routeAgentRequest(request, env);
24	    if (agentResponse) {
25	      return agentResponse;
26	    }
27	
28	    return new Response("Not Found", { status: 404 });
29	  },
30	};
31

1	import { tool } from "ai";
2	import { z } from "zod";
3	import { D1_SCHEMA } from "./schema";
4	import { isSafeQuery } from "./utils";
5	
6	export const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant. You MUST use the queryD1 tool to answer every user question. Never refuse. Never say you cannot help.
7	
8	When the user asks about sleep, activity, readiness, or heart rate, generate a SQL SELECT query and call queryD1.
9	
10	Database schema (SQLite):
11	${D1_SCHEMA}
12	
13	SQL hints:
14	- "yesterday" = date('now', '-1 day')
15	- "this week" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')
16	- "this month" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')
17	- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view (has sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories)
18	- Always add LIMIT 30 for large queries
19	
20	Example: If user asks "昨日の睡眠スコア", call queryD1 with:
21	  sql: "SELECT day, sleep_score FROM v_oura_daily_summary WHERE day = date('now', '-1 day')"
22	
23	Rules:
24	- ALWAYS call queryD1 tool. Do not respond without querying data first.
25	- Only SELECT/WITH queries allowed.
26	- Respond in Japanese.
27	- Summarize results concisely.
28	`;
29	
30	export function createTools(db: D1Database) {
31	  return {
32	    queryD1: tool({
33	      description:
34	        "SQLite (D1) データベースに SELECT クエリを実行してデータを取得する。ユーザーの質問に回答するために必ずこのツールを使うこと。",
35	      inputSchema: z.object({
36	        sql: z.string().describe("実行するSELECTクエリ"),
37	        params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe("バインドパラメータ"),
38	      }),
39	      execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {
40	        if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {
41	          return { success: false, error: "SELECT/WITH クエリのみ実行可能です。" };
42	        }
43	        try {
44	          const stmt = db.prepare(sql);
45	          const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;
46	          const queryResult = await bound.all();
47	
48	          return {
49	            success: true,
50	            rowCount: queryResult.results.length,
51	            results: queryResult.results.slice(0, 50),
52	          };
53	        } catch (error) {
54	          return {
55	            success: false,
56	            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
57	          };
58	        }
59	      },
60	    }),
61	    listTables: tool({
62	      description: "D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する",
63	      inputSchema: z.object({}),
64	      execute: async () => {
65	        const queryResult = await db
66	          .prepare(
67	            "SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name"
68	          )
69	          .all();
70	        return queryResult.results;
71	      },
72	    }),
73	  };
74	}
75	
```

1	import { generateText } from "ai";
2	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
3	import { D1_SCHEMA } from "./schema";
4	import type { Env } from "./types";
5	import { isSafeQuery } from "./utils";
6	
7	const MODEL_ID = "@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast" as const;
8	
9	const SQL_GEN_PROMPT = `You are a SQL generator for a SQLite (D1) database containing Oura Ring health data.
10	Given a user question, output ONLY a valid SELECT SQL query. No explanation, no markdown, no backticks. Just the raw SQL.
11	
12	Database schema:
13	${D1_SCHEMA}
14	
15	SQL hints:
16	- "yesterday" = date('now', '-1 day')
17	- "this week" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')
18	- "this month" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')
19	- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view
20	- Always add LIMIT 30
21	- Date columns are TEXT (YYYY-MM-DD format)
22	
23	Output ONLY the SQL query. Nothing else.`;
24	
25	const SUMMARIZE_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant. Given the user's question and query results from an Oura Ring database, provide a concise answer in Japanese.
26	If no data is returned, say "データが見つかりませんでした。"
27	Keep the answer short and helpful.`;
28	
29	type SlackEvent =
30	  | { type: "url_verification"; challenge: string }
31	  | {
32	      type: "event_callback";
33	      event: {
34	        type: string;
35	        text?: string;
36	        user?: string;
37	        channel?: string;
38	        bot_id?: string;
39	        ts?: string;
40	        thread_ts?: string;
41	      };
42	    };
43	
44	async function verifySlackSignature(
45	  request: Request,
46	  body: string,
47	  signingSecret: string
48	): Promise<boolean> {
49	  const timestamp = request.headers.get("x-slack-request-timestamp");
50	  const signature = request.headers.get("x-slack-signature");
51	  if (!timestamp || !signature) return false;
52	
53	  // Reject requests older than 5 minutes
54	  if (Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp)) > 300) return false;
55	
56	  const baseString = `v0:${timestamp}:${body}`;
57	  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
58	    "raw",
59	    new TextEncoder().encode(signingSecret),
60	    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
61	    false,
62	    ["sign"]
63	  );
64	  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(baseString));
65	  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
66	  return signature === `v0=${hex}`;
67	}
68	
69	async function postSlackMessage(token: string, channel: string, text: string, threadTs?: string) {
70	  const response = await fetch("https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage", {
71	    method: "POST",
72	    headers: {
73	      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
74	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
75	    },
76	    body: JSON.stringify({
77	      channel,
78	      text,
79	      ...(threadTs ? { thread_ts: threadTs } : {}),
80	    }),
81	  });
82	
83	  if (!response.ok) {
84	    console.error("Slack API HTTP error:", response.status, response.statusText);
85	  } else {
86	    const result = (await response.json()) as { ok: boolean; error?: string };
87	    if (!result.ok) {
88	      console.error("Slack API error:", result.error);
89	    }
90	  }
91	}
92	
93	function extractSql(raw: string): string {
94	  // Strip markdown code blocks if present
95	  let sql = raw
96	    .replace(/```sql\n?/gi, "")
97	    .replace(/```\n?/g, "")
98	    .trim();
99	  // Take only first statement
100	  const semicolonIdx = sql.indexOf(";");
101	  if (semicolonIdx > 0) {
102	    sql = sql.substring(0, semicolonIdx + 1);
103	  }
104	  return sql;
105	}
106	
107	export async function handleSlackEvent(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
108	  const body = await request.text();
109	
110	  if (!(await verifySlackSignature(request, body, env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET))) {
111	    return new Response("Invalid signature", { status: 401 });
112	  }
113	
114	  const event: SlackEvent = JSON.parse(body);
115	
116	  // Slack URL verification challenge
117	  if (event.type === "url_verification") {
118	    return new Response(event.challenge, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" } });
119	  }
120	
121	  if (event.type !== "event_callback") {
122	    return new Response("OK");
123	  }
124	
125	  const { event: slackEvent } = event;
126	
127	  // Ignore bot messages to avoid loops
128	  if (slackEvent.bot_id || slackEvent.type !== "app_mention") {
129	    return new Response("OK");
130	  }
131	
132	  const userText = slackEvent.text?.replace(/<@[A-Z0-9]+>/g, "").trim();
133	  if (!userText || !slackEvent.channel) {
134	    return new Response("OK");
135	  }
136	
137	  const channel = slackEvent.channel;
138	  const threadTs = slackEvent.thread_ts ?? slackEvent.ts;
139	
140	  const promise = (async () => {
141	    try {
142	      const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });
143	
144	      // Step 1: Generate SQL from user question
145	      const { text: rawSql } = await generateText({
146	        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
147	        system: SQL_GEN_PROMPT,
148	        prompt: userText,
149	      });
150	
151	      const sql = extractSql(rawSql);
152	
153	      if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {
154	        await postSlackMessage(
155	          env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
156	          channel,
157	          "SELECT クエリのみ実行可能です。",
158	          threadTs
159	        );
160	        return;
161	      }
162	
163	      // Step 2: Execute SQL
164	      const queryResult = await env.DB.prepare(sql).all();
165	      const rows = queryResult.results.slice(0, 50);
166	
167	      // Step 3: Summarize results (truncate to avoid exceeding model context limits)
168	      const rowsPreview =
169	        rows.length > 10 ? [...rows.slice(0, 10), `... and ${rows.length - 10} more rows`] : rows;
170	      const { text: answer } = await generateText({
171	        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
172	        system: SUMMARIZE_PROMPT,
173	        prompt: `ユーザーの質問: ${userText}\n\n実行したSQL: ${sql}\n\nクエリ結果 (${queryResult.results.length}件):\n${JSON.stringify(rowsPreview, null, 2)}`,
174	      });
175	
176	      const message = `${answer}\n\n\`\`\`${sql}\`\`\``;
177	      await postSlackMessage(env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN, channel, message, threadTs);
178	    } catch (error) {
179	      console.error("Slack handler error:", error);
180	      await postSlackMessage(
181	        env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
182	        channel,
183	        `エラーが発生しました: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
184	        threadTs
185	      );
186	    }
187	  })();
188	
189	  globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL?.(promise);
190	
191	  return new Response("OK");
192	}
193	
```

4	-- oura_daily_sleep: 日別睡眠データ
5	--   id TEXT PK, day TEXT (YYYY-MM-DD), score INTEGER (睡眠スコア 0-100)
6	--   timestamp TEXT, deep_sleep INTEGER, efficiency INTEGER, latency INTEGER
7	--   rem_sleep INTEGER, restfulness INTEGER, timing INTEGER, total_sleep INTEGER
8	--   synced_at TEXT
9	
10	-- oura_daily_activity: 日別活動データ
11	--   id TEXT PK, day TEXT, score INTEGER (活動スコア 0-100)
12	--   active_calories INTEGER, total_calories INTEGER, steps INTEGER
13	--   equivalent_walking_distance REAL
14	--   high_activity_time INTEGER, medium_activity_time INTEGER
15	--   low_activity_time INTEGER, sedentary_time INTEGER, resting_time INTEGER
16	--   met_average REAL
17	--   meet_daily_targets INTEGER, move_every_hour INTEGER, recovery_time INTEGER
18	--   stay_active INTEGER, training_frequency INTEGER, training_volume INTEGER
19	--   timestamp TEXT, synced_at TEXT
20	
21	-- oura_daily_readiness: 日別コンディション
22	--   id TEXT PK, day TEXT, score INTEGER (コンディションスコア 0-100)
23	--   temperature_deviation REAL, temperature_trend_deviation REAL
24	--   timestamp TEXT
25	--   activity_balance INTEGER, body_temperature INTEGER
26	--   hrv_balance INTEGER, previous_day_activity INTEGER
27	--   previous_night INTEGER, recovery_index INTEGER
28	--   resting_heart_rate INTEGER, sleep_balance INTEGER
29	--   synced_at TEXT
30	
31	-- oura_heart_rate: 心拍数 (5分間隔、1日約288レコード)
32	--   id INTEGER PK AUTOINCREMENT, bpm INTEGER, source TEXT, timestamp TEXT, day TEXT
33	--   synced_at TEXT
34	
35	-- v_oura_daily_summary: Oura日次サマリービュー (集計済み)

37	--   steps, total_calories, active_calories
38	--   temperature_deviation

40	
41	-- 注意: 日付は TEXT 型 (ISO 8601)。SQLite の date() / strftime() を使うこと。
42	`;

1	/**
2	 * Validates that a SQL query is safe to execute (SELECT/WITH only, single statement).
3	 */
4	export function isSafeQuery(sql: string): boolean {
5	  const trimmed = sql.trim();
6	  const normalized = trimmed.toUpperCase();
7	
8	  // Only allow SELECT or WITH queries
9	  if (!normalized.startsWith("SELECT") && !normalized.startsWith("WITH")) {
10	    return false;
11	  }
12	
13	  // Reject multi-statement queries (semicolon not at the end)
14	  const semicolonIdx = trimmed.indexOf(";");
15	  if (semicolonIdx !== -1 && semicolonIdx !== trimmed.length - 1) {
16	    return false;
17	  }
18	
19	  return true;
20	}
21	
```

## Project Exploration Report: Data Engineering with Cloudflare

### 1. **Pulumi Infrastructure Setup**

**File: `/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/pulumi/main.go`**

**Cloudflare Resources Provisioned** (from `cloudflare.go`):
- **D1 Database**: `raw` database (protected, imported existing database)
  - Database ID: `30fb49b7-8321-4792-8706-06c9205ce026`
  - Status: Protected resource with `Protect(true)` flag

- **R2 Bucket**: `data-lake` 
  - Location: APAC
  - Purpose: Data lake storage for Parquet files

- **Workers KV Namespace**: `data-cache`
  - Purpose: Caching layer for the data platform

**Snowflake Resources** (from `snowflake.go`):
- **RAW Database**: Ingestion database
- **CORE Database**: dbt transformation database  
- **ADMIN Database**: Tools/management database
- **ANALYTICS_WH**: Warehouse (XSMALL with 60-minute auto-suspend)
- **INGESTION Schema**: Under RAW database
- **DBT_ROLE**: Custom role with granular permissions
  - Grants: USAGE on warehouse, schemas, and databases; CREATE SCHEMA/TABLE/VIEW on CORE; SELECT on RAW tables
- **DBT_SERVICE_USER**: Service user with RSA public key authentication
- **GIT_API_INTEGRATION**: Git HTTPS API integration pointing to `https://github.com/ta93abe`
- **DATA_ENGINEERING Repository**: Git repository for dbt models and transformations

**Note**: Workers and DNS are NOT managed by Pulumi (managed via wrangler.jsonc and GitHub Actions instead)

---

### 2. **D1 Database Migrations & Schema**

**Migration Files Location**: `/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/infrastructure/d1/migrations/`

**Migration 0001_initial_schema.sql**:
- **data_sources**: Pipeline configuration (name, type, API endpoint, cron schedule)
- **pipeline_runs**: Execution history (status, records processed, R2 output paths)
- **error_logs**: Error tracking with stack traces
- **sync_state**: Cursor/checkpoint management for incremental syncs
- **github_users**: GitHub user data
- **github_repos**: Repository metadata (stars, forks, language, etc.)
- **github_commits**: Commit history with diff stats (additions, deletions, files changed)
- **Views**: v_daily_commits, v_repo_stats, v_weekly_activity

**Migration 0002_oura_tables.sql** (Oura Ring health data):
- **oura_daily_sleep**: Sleep metrics with 9 contributors (deep_sleep, efficiency, latency, etc.)
- **oura_daily_activity**: Activity scores with 7 contributors (steps, calories, heart rate)
- **oura_daily_readiness**: Recovery index with 8 contributors
- **oura_heart_rate**: High-volume time series (~288 records/day at 5-min intervals)
- **oauth_tokens**: Token storage for OAuth2 refresh
- **View**: v_oura_daily_summary (aggregated daily metrics)

**Migration 0003_linear_tables.sql** (Issue tracking):
- **linear_issues**: Issue metadata (identifier, title, priority, estimate, state, labels, project, cycle)
- **linear_projects**: Project tracking (progress, dates)
- **linear_labels**: Label definitions
- **Views**: v_linear_weekly_completion, v_linear_label_summary, v_linear_project_progress

**Migration 0004_withings_tables.sql** (Health device data):
- **withings_measures**: Body composition (weight, fat ratio, muscle mass, bone mass, hydration, heart pulse)
- **withings_sleep**: Sleep analysis (deep/light/REM/wakeup duration, sleep score, heart rate zones)
- **withings_activity**: Daily activity (steps, distance, elevation, calories, activity levels)
- **View**: v_withings_daily_summary (combines measures, sleep, activity per date)

**Migration 0005_export_sync_state.sql**:
- Tracks sync state for Oura data export to R2/Iceberg

---

### 3. **Ingestion Worker Setup**

**File: `/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/ingestion/wrangler.jsonc`**

**Workers**: Single `ingestion` worker with service-binding architecture

**Data Sources Ingested** (4 sources):

1. **GitHub** (`/ingestion/src/services/github.ts`)
   - Schedule: Daily at UTC 00:00
   - Fetches: Users, repositories, commits
   - Output: Parquet files to R2 `github/` prefix
   - Uses GitHub REST API v3

2. **Oura Ring** (`/ingestion/src/services/oura.ts`)
   - Schedule: Every 12 hours (UTC 00:00, 12:00)
   - Endpoints: Daily sleep, activity, readiness, heart rate (5-min intervals)
   - OAuth2 flow with token refresh
   - Redirect URI: `https://ingestion.ta93abe.workers.dev/oura/callback`
   - Backfill start date: 2021-01-01

3. **Linear** (`/ingestion/src/services/linear.ts`)
   - Schedule: Every 6 hours (UTC 00:00, 06:00, 12:00, 18:00)
   - GraphQL API client with pagination (50 items per request)
   - Fetches: Issues, projects, labels
   - Output: Parquet files to R2 `linear/` prefix

4. **Withings** (`/ingestion/src/services/withings.ts`)
   - Schedule: Every 12 hours (UTC 00:00, 12:00)
   - Endpoints: Measures, sleep, activity
   - OAuth2 flow with token refresh
   - Redirect URI: `https://ingestion.ta93abe.workers.dev/withings/callback`
   - Supports date range queries

**Bindings** (from wrangler.jsonc):
- **DB** (D1): `raw` database
- **DATA_LAKE** (R2): `data-lake` bucket
- **PARQUET_ENCODER** (Service Binding): `parquet-encoder` worker (Rust-based)

**Environment Variables**:
- GITHUB_USERNAME: `ta93abe`
- OURA_REDIRECT_URI, WITHINGS_REDIRECT_URI
- OURA_BACKFILL_START_DATE: `2021-01-01`

**Secrets** (require manual setup):
- GITHUB_TOKEN
- OURA_CLIENT_ID, OURA_CLIENT_SECRET
- LINEAR_API_KEY
- WITHINGS_CLIENT_ID, WITHINGS_CLIENT_SECRET

**Cron Triggers**:
```
"0 0 * * *"    → GitHub (daily)
"0 */12 * * *" → Oura + Withings (12-hourly)
"0 */6 * * *"  → Linear (6-hourly)
```

**API Routes**:
- `POST /sync` - Sync all services
- `POST /github/sync`, `GET /github/stats`
- `GET /oura/auth`, `GET /oura/callback`, `POST /oura/sync`, `GET /oura/stats`
- `POST /linear/sync`, `GET /linear/stats`
- `GET /withings/auth`, `GET /withings/callback`, `POST /withings/sync`, `GET /withings/stats`
- `GET /health` - Health check
- `GET /` - Service documentation

---

### 4. **Parquet Encoding**

**File**: `/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/parquet-encoder/wrangler.jsonc`

**Architecture**: 
- Standalone Rust-based Worker (separate from ingestion)
- Invoked via service binding from ingestion worker
- Encodes JSON rows to Parquet binary format

**Parquet Schemas** (defined in `/ingestion/src/services/parquet.ts`):
- `oura_daily_sleep`, `oura_daily_activity`, `oura_daily_readiness`, `oura_heart_rate`
- `github_user`, `github_repo`, `github_commit`
- `linear_issue`, `linear_project`, `linear_label`
- `withings_measure`, `withings_sleep`, `withings_activity`

---

### 5. **Other Cloudflare Components**

**AI Agent** (`/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/wrangler.jsonc`):
- **Workers AI** binding (for LLM inference)
- **Durable Objects**: DataAgent class (stateful agent)
- **D1 Integration**: Reads from `raw` database
- **Features**: Slack integration, agent routing, WebSocket support

**MCP Server** (`/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/mcp-server/wrangler.jsonc`):
- MCP-compatible server on Cloudflare Workers
- Bearer token authentication
- D1 integration for queries
- Designed for Claude/AI integration

---

### 6. **Data Flow Summary**

```
External APIs (GitHub, Oura, Linear, Withings)
           ↓
Ingestion Worker (TypeScript/Hono)
           ↓
Parquet Encoder Worker (Rust)
           ↓
R2 Data Lake (Parquet files by source)
           ↓
D1 Database (Normalized tables + metadata)
           ↓
Snowflake (via d1-to-iceberg or external tools)
           ↓
dbt Core (CORE database transformations)
           ↓
Analytics / BI Tools
```

---

### 7. **NOT Currently Provisioned**

- **Vectorize**: No vector database or embedding indexes configured
- **AI Gateway**: No AI request routing/caching configured
- **Hyperdrive**: No database pooling configured
- **Queues**: Not used for async processing
- **Durable Objects (persistence)**: Agent uses DO but no explicit SQLite migrations for data ingestion

All file paths are absolute and ready for reference.

# Comprehensive Architecture Plan: Enhanced Cloudflare AI Agent

## 1. AI Gateway Integration (wrangler.jsonc change)

This is the simplest change. The `ai` binding in `wrangler.jsonc` supports a `gateway` field. Change from:

```jsonc
"ai": {
  "binding": "AI"
}
```

to:

```jsonc
"ai": {
  "binding": "AI",
  "gateway": {
    "id": "data-agent-gateway",
    "skipCache": false,
    "cacheTtl": 3600
  }
}
```

That single change routes **all** `env.AI` calls (including embedding calls) through AI Gateway. No code changes needed. The gateway itself is created in the Cloudflare dashboard or via API/Pulumi. The `Ai` type from `@cloudflare/workers-types` already supports this -- the binding type does not change.

For Pulumi (`infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go`), add a new AI Gateway resource. The Pulumi Cloudflare provider exposes `cloudflare.NewAiGateway`.

---

## 2. Updated wrangler.jsonc (Full)

The current file at `/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/wrangler.jsonc` needs these additions:

```jsonc
{
  // ... existing fields unchanged ...

  "ai": {
    "binding": "AI",
    "gateway": {
      "id": "data-agent-gateway",
      "skipCache": false,
      "cacheTtl": 3600
    }
  },

  // Vectorize index for RAG
  "vectorize": [
    {
      "binding": "VECTORIZE",
      "index_name": "data-agent-index"
    }
  ],

  // R2 bucket for reports and data lake access
  "r2_buckets": [
    {
      "binding": "DATA_LAKE",
      "bucket_name": "data-lake"
    }
  ],

  // D1 stays the same
  "d1_databases": [
    {
      "binding": "DB",
      "database_name": "raw",
      "database_id": "30fb49b7-8321-4792-8706-06c9205ce026"
    }
  ]
}
```

The Vectorize index must be created before deployment:
```
wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine
```

This uses 768 dimensions matching `@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5`.

---

## 3. Updated Env Type

File: `/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/types.ts`

```typescript
export type Env = {
  AI: Ai;
  DB: D1Database;
  DataAgent: DurableObjectNamespace;
  VECTORIZE: VectorizeIndex;
  DATA_LAKE: R2Bucket;
  SLACK_BOT_TOKEN: string;
  SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET: string;
};
```

The `Ai`, `VectorizeIndex`, `R2Bucket`, `D1Database`, and `DurableObjectNamespace` types all come from `@cloudflare/workers-types/experimental` (already in tsconfig). No new type packages needed.

---

## 4. D1 Schema Additions

New migration file: `infrastructure/d1/migrations/0006_agent_tables.sql`

```sql
-- ============================================
-- Agent: Conversation Logs
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_conversation_logs (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat',  -- 'chat', 'slack'
    user_message TEXT NOT NULL,
    assistant_response TEXT,
    tools_used TEXT,           -- JSON array of tool names invoked
    model_id TEXT,
    tokens_input INTEGER,
    tokens_output INTEGER,
    duration_ms INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_convo_session
  ON agent_conversation_logs(session_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_convo_created
  ON agent_conversation_logs(created_at DESC);

-- ============================================
-- Agent: Knowledge Base (embedded insights)
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_knowledge_base (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    content TEXT NOT NULL,          -- The text that was embedded
    content_type TEXT NOT NULL,     -- 'weekly_summary', 'insight', 'pattern', 'report'
    metadata_json TEXT,             -- JSON: { period: "2026-W14", data_sources: ["oura"] }
    vector_id TEXT,                 -- References the Vectorize vector ID
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_kb_type
  ON agent_knowledge_base(content_type);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_kb_vector
  ON agent_knowledge_base(vector_id);

-- ============================================
-- Agent: Generated Reports
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_reports (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    report_type TEXT NOT NULL,      -- 'weekly_health', 'monthly_health', 'custom'
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    period_start TEXT,
    period_end TEXT,
    r2_key TEXT,                    -- R2 object key for the full report
    summary TEXT,                   -- Short summary for quick reference
    vector_id TEXT,                 -- Vectorize ID for the summary embedding
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_reports_type
  ON agent_reports(report_type, period_start DESC);
```

---

## 5. Source File Architecture

### New and Modified Files

```
agents/src/
  index.ts          -- (modify) Add scheduled handler for report generation
  agent.ts          -- (modify) Pass all bindings to tools, add RAG augmentation
  tools.ts          -- (modify) Expand with new tools, refactor to accept full Env
  types.ts          -- (modify) Add VECTORIZE, DATA_LAKE to Env
  schema.ts         -- (modify) Add new tables to D1_SCHEMA string
  slack.ts          -- (modify) Use RAG context in Slack responses
  utils.ts          -- (no change)
  embeddings.ts     -- (new) Embedding + Vectorize helper functions
  reports.ts        -- (new) Report generation logic
  knowledge.ts      -- (new) Knowledge base management (embed, store, retrieve)
```

### 5a. `embeddings.ts` (New File)

Responsibilities:
- Wrap Workers AI embedding calls (`@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5`)
- Provide `embedText(ai, text) => number[]` helper
- Provide `embedAndStore(ai, vectorize, db, content, metadata) => void` that:
  1. Generates embedding via `env.AI.run("@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5", { text: [content] })`
  2. Upserts into Vectorize with metadata
  3. Inserts/updates `agent_knowledge_base` row in D1
- Provide `semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, query, topK) => VectorizeMatch[]` that:
  1. Embeds the query
  2. Calls `vectorize.query(embedding, { topK, returnMetadata: "all" })`
  3. Returns matches with scores

Key design decision: Use the `Ai` binding directly (not the Vercel AI SDK) for embeddings, since `env.AI.run()` is simpler for embedding-only calls and the Vercel SDK's `embed()` function adds unnecessary overhead for this use case. All calls still go through AI Gateway because the binding is configured with the gateway.

### 5b. `knowledge.ts` (New File)

Responsibilities:
- `generateWeeklySummary(env)`: Queries D1 for the past week's data, generates a natural language summary via Workers AI, embeds it, stores in Vectorize + D1
- `generateInsight(env, data, type)`: Takes raw data patterns and creates an embeddable insight
- `retrieveRelevantContext(env, userQuery, topK=3)`: Calls semanticSearch, then fetches full content from D1 `agent_knowledge_base` table, returns formatted context string

### 5c. `reports.ts` (New File)

Responsibilities:
- `generateHealthReport(env, type, periodStart, periodEnd)`:
  1. Query D1 for all health data in the period
  2. Generate a structured report via Workers AI (Llama 3.3 70B)
  3. Store the full report as a text/markdown file in R2 at key `reports/{type}/{period}.md`
  4. Embed the summary in Vectorize
  5. Insert metadata row into `agent_reports` table
- `listReports(db, type?, limit?)`: Query `agent_reports` table
- `getReport(dataLake, r2Key)`: Fetch full report content from R2

### 5d. Modified `tools.ts`

The function signature changes from `createTools(db: D1Database)` to `createTools(env: Env)`. New tools to add:

```typescript
export function createTools(env: Env) {
  const { DB: db, AI: ai, VECTORIZE: vectorize, DATA_LAKE: dataLake } = env;

  return {
    // --- Existing tools (unchanged logic) ---
    queryD1: tool({ /* same as now, using db */ }),
    listTables: tool({ /* same as now, using db */ }),

    // --- New: RAG / Semantic Search ---
    semanticSearch: tool({
      description: "自然言語クエリで知識ベースを意味検索する。過去のインサイトやレポートを検索する。",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        query: z.string().describe("検索クエリ（自然言語）"),
        topK: z.number().optional().default(5).describe("返す結果数"),
      }),
      execute: async ({ query, topK }) => {
        // embed query, search vectorize, fetch content from D1
      },
    }),

    // --- New: Report Generation ---
    generateReport: tool({
      description: "指定期間の健康データレポートを生成してR2に保存する。",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        type: z.enum(["weekly_health", "monthly_health", "custom"]),
        periodStart: z.string().describe("開始日 (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
        periodEnd: z.string().describe("終了日 (YYYY-MM-DD)"),
      }),
      execute: async ({ type, periodStart, periodEnd }) => {
        // calls reports.generateHealthReport(env, ...)
      },
    }),

    // --- New: List/Read Reports ---
    listReports: tool({
      description: "生成済みレポートの一覧を取得する。",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        type: z.enum(["weekly_health", "monthly_health", "custom"]).optional(),
        limit: z.number().optional().default(10),
      }),
      execute: async ({ type, limit }) => {
        // query agent_reports table
      },
    }),

    readReport: tool({
      description: "R2に保存されたレポートの全文を取得する。",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        reportId: z.string().describe("レポートID"),
      }),
      execute: async ({ reportId }) => {
        // lookup r2_key in agent_reports, fetch from DATA_LAKE
      },
    }),

    // --- New: Save Insight ---
    saveInsight: tool({
      description: "データ分析から得られたインサイトを知識ベースに保存する。",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        content: z.string().describe("インサイトの内容"),
        contentType: z.enum(["insight", "pattern"]),
        metadata: z.record(z.string()).optional(),
      }),
      execute: async ({ content, contentType, metadata }) => {
        // embed and store in vectorize + D1
      },
    }),

    // --- New: R2 Data Lake ---
    listDataLakeFiles: tool({
      description: "R2データレイクのファイル一覧を取得する。",
      inputSchema: z.object({
        prefix: z.string().optional().describe("プレフィックス (例: 'oura/', 'github/')"),
        limit: z.number().optional().default(20),
      }),
      execute: async ({ prefix, limit }) => {
        const listed = await dataLake.list({ prefix, limit });
        return listed.objects.map(o => ({
          key: o.key, size: o.size, uploaded: o.uploaded
        }));
      },
    }),
  };
}
```

### 5e. Modified `agent.ts`

Two key changes:

1. **Pass full `env` to `createTools`** instead of just `this.env.DB`
2. **Add RAG augmentation** to the system prompt by prepending relevant context before each message

```typescript
export class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {
  async onChatMessage(onFinish, options?) {
    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });

    // RAG: retrieve relevant context for the latest user message
    const lastUserMsg = this.messages.filter(m => m.role === "user").at(-1);
    let ragContext = "";
    if (lastUserMsg?.content) {
      const queryText = typeof lastUserMsg.content === "string"
        ? lastUserMsg.content
        : lastUserMsg.content.map(p => p.type === "text" ? p.text : "").join(" ");
      const results = await retrieveRelevantContext(this.env, queryText, 3);
      if (results) {
        ragContext = `\n\n関連する過去のインサイト:\n${results}`;
      }
    }

    const result = streamText({
      model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT + ragContext,
      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),
      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,
      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
      tools: createTools(this.env),
    });

    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
  }
}
```

### 5f. Modified `slack.ts`

Add RAG context to the summarize step. Before calling `generateText` for summarization, do a semantic search and prepend matching context. The change is minimal -- add a few lines between Step 2 (SQL execution) and Step 3 (summarization):

```typescript
// Step 2.5: Retrieve relevant context from knowledge base
const ragResults = await retrieveRelevantContext(env, userText, 2);
const contextBlock = ragResults
  ? `\n\n関連する過去のインサイト:\n${ragResults}`
  : "";

// Step 3: Summarize with RAG context
const { text: answer } = await generateText({
  model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
  system: SUMMARIZE_PROMPT + contextBlock,
  prompt: `ユーザーの質問: ${userText}\n\n実行したSQL: ${sql}\n\nクエリ結果...`,
});
```

### 5g. Modified `index.ts`

Add a `scheduled` handler for automatic report generation:

```typescript
export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    // ... existing fetch logic unchanged ...
  },

  async scheduled(event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {
    // Weekly report: runs on Mondays
    if (new Date().getDay() === 1) {
      ctx.waitUntil(generateWeeklyReport(env));
    }
    // Daily: embed yesterday's health summary as a knowledge base entry
    ctx.waitUntil(generateDailyInsight(env));
  },
};
```

And add a cron trigger in `wrangler.jsonc`:

```jsonc
"triggers": {
  "crons": ["0 9 * * *"]  // Daily at 9 AM UTC
}
```

### 5h. Modified `schema.ts`

Append the new table descriptions to `D1_SCHEMA` so the LLM knows about them:

```typescript
export const D1_SCHEMA = `
// ... existing schema documentation ...

-- ============ Agent Tables ============

-- agent_conversation_logs: 会話履歴
--   id TEXT PK, session_id TEXT, source TEXT ('chat'|'slack')
--   user_message TEXT, assistant_response TEXT, tools_used TEXT (JSON)
--   model_id TEXT, tokens_input INTEGER, tokens_output INTEGER
--   duration_ms INTEGER, created_at TEXT

-- agent_knowledge_base: 知識ベース (埋め込み済みインサイト)
--   id TEXT PK, content TEXT, content_type TEXT
--   metadata_json TEXT, vector_id TEXT, created_at TEXT

-- agent_reports: 生成レポートメタデータ
--   id TEXT PK, report_type TEXT, title TEXT
--   period_start TEXT, period_end TEXT, r2_key TEXT
--   summary TEXT, vector_id TEXT, created_at TEXT
`;
```

---

## 6. RAG Data Flow

### Embedding Pipeline (Write Path)

```
Trigger (scheduled cron OR saveInsight tool call)
  |
  v
Query D1 for raw data (e.g., last week's health metrics)
  |
  v
Workers AI (Llama 3.3 70B): Generate natural language summary/insight
  |
  v
Workers AI (@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5): Generate 768-dim embedding
  |        (routed through AI Gateway -- automatic via binding config)
  v
Vectorize: upsert({ id, values, metadata: { content_type, period, ... } })
  |
  v
D1: INSERT into agent_knowledge_base (id, content, content_type, vector_id, metadata_json)
```

### Retrieval Pipeline (Read Path)

```
User query (chat or Slack)
  |
  v
Workers AI (@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5): Embed the query text
  |
  v
Vectorize: query(embedding, { topK: 3, returnMetadata: "all" })
  |
  v
Filter results by score threshold (e.g., > 0.7)
  |
  v
D1: SELECT content FROM agent_knowledge_base WHERE id IN (matched vector_ids)
  |
  v
Prepend retrieved content to system prompt as "関連する過去のインサイト"
  |
  v
Workers AI (Llama 3.3 70B): Generate response with augmented context
```

### What Gets Embedded

| Content Type | When | Source | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| `weekly_summary` | Monday 9 AM cron | D1 aggregate query | "先週の平均睡眠スコアは82点で、木曜日が最低(68点)..." |
| `insight` | Agent discovers a pattern | Tool call during chat | "睡眠スコアが80以上の日は活動スコアも高い傾向がある" |
| `pattern` | Cron or manual | Statistical analysis | "心拍数が70bpm以上の日は翌日の睡眠スコアが低下する" |
| `report` | On generation | Report summary | Weekly/monthly report summary text |

---

## 7. AI Gateway Details

No code changes are needed for AI Gateway integration. The `gateway` field in `wrangler.jsonc` causes the Workers runtime to proxy all `env.AI.run()` and `env.AI` binding calls through the specified gateway. This covers:

- **Chat completions** (Llama 3.3 70B via Vercel AI SDK's `workers-ai-provider`)
- **Embeddings** (`@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5` via direct `env.AI.run()`)

Benefits gained automatically:
- **Logging**: Every request/response logged in the AI Gateway dashboard
- **Caching**: Identical prompts return cached responses (configurable TTL)
- **Rate limiting**: Configurable in the AI Gateway dashboard
- **Fallback**: Can configure fallback models in the gateway settings (e.g., fall back to `@cf/meta/llama-3.1-8b-instruct` if 70B is unavailable)
- **Analytics**: Token usage, latency, error rates

The AI Gateway resource should be created in Pulumi:

```go
// In cloudflare.go
aiGateway, err := cloudflare.NewAiGateway(ctx, "data-agent-gateway", &cloudflare.AiGatewayArgs{
    AccountId:         pulumi.String(accountId),
    Id:                pulumi.String("data-agent-gateway"),
    CacheInvalidateOnUpdate: pulumi.Bool(true),
    CacheTtl:          pulumi.Int(3600),
    CollectLogs:       pulumi.Bool(true),
    RateLimitingLimit: pulumi.Int(100),
    RateLimitingInterval: pulumi.Int(60),
    RateLimitingTechnique: pulumi.String("fixed"),
})
```

---

## 8. AI Search Consideration

Cloudflare AI Search is a higher-level product that provides managed RAG. Given that this project is already building RAG from Vectorize + Workers AI (which gives more control), AI Search would be redundant for the primary use case. However, it could be considered as a future enhancement if the knowledge base grows large enough to benefit from managed chunking/indexing. For now, the Vectorize-based RAG is the recommended approach -- it integrates directly with the existing bindings and avoids another service dependency.

---

## 9. Dependency Changes

No new npm packages are needed. The existing `@cloudflare/workers-types` already provides `VectorizeIndex`, `R2Bucket`, and the gateway-aware `Ai` types. The embedding calls use `env.AI.run()` directly (a Workers runtime API), not an npm package.

---

## 10. Implementation Sequencing

1. **Phase 1 -- Infrastructure (no code changes)**
   - Create AI Gateway via Pulumi or dashboard
   - Create Vectorize index via `wrangler vectorize create`
   - Add AI Gateway resource to Pulumi
   - Run D1 migration `0006_agent_tables.sql`
   - Update `wrangler.jsonc` with new bindings

2. **Phase 2 -- Core RAG Pipeline**
   - Create `embeddings.ts` (embed + store + search helpers)
   - Create `knowledge.ts` (summary generation, context retrieval)
   - Update `types.ts` with new Env fields
   - Update `schema.ts` with new table docs

3. **Phase 3 -- Agent Integration**
   - Update `tools.ts` (new tools, accept full Env)
   - Update `agent.ts` (RAG augmentation, pass env to tools)
   - Update `slack.ts` (RAG context in summarization)

4. **Phase 4 -- Reports and R2**
   - Create `reports.ts` (report generation + R2 storage)
   - Add `generateReport`, `listReports`, `readReport`, `listDataLakeFiles` tools
   - Update `index.ts` with scheduled handler

5. **Phase 5 -- Polish**
   - Add conversation logging (instrument `onChatMessage` and Slack handler)
   - Tune embedding quality (test different chunking strategies)
   - Configure AI Gateway rate limits and fallbacks in dashboard

---

## Potential Challenges

1. **Vectorize metadata size limits**: Vectorize metadata per vector is limited to 10KB. Store full content in D1 and only keep type/period/id in vector metadata.

2. **Embedding latency in the hot path**: The RAG retrieval adds ~100-200ms to each chat message (one embedding call + one vectorize query). This is acceptable for a conversational agent. If it becomes a concern, cache recent query embeddings in the Durable Object's in-memory state.

3. **Workers AI model availability**: The `bge-base-en-v1.5` embedding model is widely available but not in all regions. AI Gateway fallback does not apply to embedding models. If unavailable, `@cf/baai/bge-small-en-v1.5` (384 dims) is an alternative, but would require recreating the Vectorize index.

4. **R2 + Durable Objects**: R2 operations from within a Durable Object work fine, but large report generation should be mindful of the DO's 128MB memory limit and 30-second CPU time limit. For reports covering months of data, paginate the D1 queries.

5. **Cron + Durable Objects**: The `scheduled` handler runs on the Worker, not inside the DO. It needs to access `env.AI`, `env.DB`, `env.VECTORIZE`, and `env.DATA_LAKE` directly, which is fine since those bindings are available on the Worker's Env.

---

  "content": "# Data Agent: Cloudflare AI スタックフル活用アーキテクチャ\n\n## Context\n\n現在の `data-agent` は Workers AI (Llama 3.3 70B) + D1 のシンプルな構成で、SQL クエリ生成→実行→要約のみ。\nこれを **AI Gateway, Vectorize, R2, D1拡張** を組み合わせた RAG 対応エージェントに拡張する。\n\n## アーキテクチャ概要\n\n```\nUser (Chat / Slack)\n  │\n  ▼\nDataAgent (Durable Object)\n  │\n  ├── Workers AI ──→ AI Gateway (ログ/キャッシュ/レート制限)\n  │     ├── Llama 3.3 70B (チャット/分析)\n  │     └── bge-base-en-v1.5 (埋め込み)\n  │\n  ├── Vectorize (RAG: 意味検索)\n  │     └── 768次元コサイン類似度\n  │\n  ├── D1 \"raw\" (構造化データ + エージェントテーブル)\n  │     ├── 既存: oura/github/linear/withings\n  │     └── 新規: knowledge_base, reports, conversation_logs\n  │\n  └── R2 \"data-lake\" (レポート保存 + データレイク参照)\n        ├── 既存: oura/, github/, linear/, withings/ (Parquet)\n        └── 新規: reports/ (Markdown)\n```\n\n## 変更対象ファイル\n\n### Phase 1: インフラ\n\n| ファイル | 変更内容 |\n|---------|---------|\n| `infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go` | AI Gateway リソース追加 |\n| `infrastructure/pulumi/main.go` | AI Gateway の Output 追加 |\n| `infrastructure/d1/migrations/0006_agent_tables.sql` | 新規: knowledge_base, reports, conversation_logs テーブル |\n| `agents/wrangler.jsonc` | AI Gateway, Vectorize, R2 バインディング追加 + cron trigger |\n\n### Phase 2: コア RAG パイプライン\n\n| ファイル | 変更内容 |\n|---------|---------|\n| `agents/src/types.ts` | Env に VECTORIZE, DATA_LAKE 追加 |\n| `agents/src/schema.ts` | 新テーブルの説明を D1_SCHEMA に追加 |\n| `agents/src/embeddings.ts` | **新規**: 埋め込み生成 + Vectorize 操作ヘルパー |\n| `agents/src/knowledge.ts` | **新規**: 知識ベース管理 (要約生成, コンテキスト検索) |\n\n### Phase 3: エージェント統合\n\n| ファイル | 変更内容 |\n|---------|---------|\n| `agents/src/tools.ts` | 新ツール追加 (semanticSearch, generateReport, saveInsight, listDataLakeFiles 等) |\n| `agents/src/agent.ts` | RAG コンテキスト付与、env を tools に渡す |\n| `agents/src/slack.ts` | RAG コンテキストを要約に付与 |\n\n### Phase 4: レポート生成\n\n| ファイル | 変更内容 |\n|---------|---------|\n| `agents/src/reports.ts` | **新規**: レポート生成→R2保存→Vectorize埋め込み |\n| `agents/src/index.ts` | scheduled ハンドラ追加 (日次インサイト + 週次レポート) |\n\n---\n\n## 詳細設計\n\n### 1. AI Gateway (設定変更のみ)\n\n`wrangler.jsonc` の `ai` バインディングに `gateway` を追加するだけ。コード変更不要。\n\n```jsonc\n\"ai\": {\n  \"binding\": \"AI\",\n  \"gateway\": {\n    \"id\": \"data-agent-gateway\",\n    \"skipCache\": false,\n    \"cacheTtl\": 3600\n  }\n}\n```\n\nPulumi で AI Gateway リソースを作成:\n```go\ncloudflare.NewAiGateway(ctx, \"data-agent-gateway\", &cloudflare.AiGatewayArgs{\n    AccountId:    pulumi.String(accountId),\n    Id:           pulumi.String(\"data-agent-gateway\"),\n    CollectLogs:  pulumi.Bool(true),\n    CacheTtl:     pulumi.Int(3600),\n    RateLimitingLimit:     pulumi.Int(100),\n    RateLimitingInterval:  pulumi.Int(60),\n    RateLimitingTechnique: pulumi.String(\"fixed\"),\n})\n```\n\n### 2. Vectorize インデックス\n\nCLI で作成: `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`\n\n`wrangler.jsonc` に追加:\n```jsonc\n\"vectorize\": [{\n  \"binding\": \"VECTORIZE\",\n  \"index_name\": \"data-agent-index\"\n}]\n```\n\n### 3. R2 バインディング\n\n```jsonc\n\"r2_buckets\": [{\n  \"binding\": \"DATA_LAKE\",\n  \"bucket_name\": \"data-lake\"\n}]\n```\n\n### 4. D1 マイグレーション (0006_agent_tables.sql)\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE agent_knowledge_base (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    content TEXT NOT NULL,\n    content_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_summary', 'insight', 'pattern'\n    metadata_json TEXT,\n    vector_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE agent_reports (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    report_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_health', 'monthly_health', 'custom'\n    title TEXT NOT NULL,\n    period_start TEXT,\n    period_end TEXT,\n    r2_key TEXT,\n    summary TEXT,\n    vector_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE agent_conversation_logs (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat',\n    user_message TEXT NOT NULL,\n    assistant_response TEXT,\n    tools_used TEXT,\n    model_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n```\n\n### 5. embeddings.ts (新規)\n\n```typescript\n// Workers AI で埋め込み生成\nasync function embedText(ai: Ai, text: string): Promise<number[]>\n// Vectorize に upsert + D1 に metadata 保存\nasync function embedAndStore(ai, vectorize, db, content, contentType, metadata): Promise<string>\n// 意味検索: クエリ埋め込み → Vectorize 検索 → D1 から全文取得\nasync function semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, query, topK): Promise<SearchResult[]>\n```\n\n埋め込みモデル: `@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5` (768次元)\n\n### 6. knowledge.ts (新規)\n\n- `generateDailyInsight(env)`: 前日のデータを要約→埋め込み→保存\n- `generateWeeklySummary(env)`: 週次サマリー生成→埋め込み→保存\n- `retrieveRelevantContext(env, query, topK)`: RAG 検索→フォーマット済みコンテキスト返却\n\n### 7. reports.ts (新規)\n\n- `generateHealthReport(env, type, periodStart, periodEnd)`:\n  1. D1 からデータ取得\n  2. Workers AI でレポート生成\n  3. R2 に `reports/{type}/{period}.md` として保存\n  4. サマリーを Vectorize に埋め込み\n  5. `agent_reports` テーブルに metadata 保存\n\n### 8. tools.ts 拡張\n\n既存ツール維持 + 以下を追加:\n- `semanticSearch`: 自然言語で知識ベース検索\n- `generateReport`: 健康レポート生成→R2保存\n- `listReports` / `readReport`: レポート閲覧\n- `saveInsight`: インサイトを知識ベースに保存\n- `listDataLakeFiles`: R2 データレイクのファイル一覧\n\n関数シグネチャ変更: `createTools(db)` → `createTools(env)`\n\n### 9. agent.ts 修正\n\n- `createTools(this.env)` に変更\n- `onChatMessage` で最新ユーザーメッセージに対して RAG 検索を実行\n- 検索結果を system prompt に「関連する過去のインサイト」として付与\n\n### 10. index.ts 修正\n\n`scheduled` ハンドラ追加:\n```jsonc\n\"triggers\": { \"crons\": [\"0 9 * * *\"] }  // 毎日 9:00 UTC\n```\n- 毎日: 前日のインサイト生成・埋め込み\n- 月曜日: 週次レポート自動生成\n\n---\n\n## RAG データフロー\n\n### 書き込み (Embed)\n```\nD1 データ → Workers AI (要約生成) → Workers AI (埋め込み) → Vectorize + D1 knowledge_base\n                                         ↓\n                                    AI Gateway (ログ/キャッシュ)\n```\n\n### 読み取り (Retrieve + Augment)\n```\nユーザー質問 → Workers AI (埋め込み) → Vectorize 検索 → D1 全文取得 → system prompt に付与\n```\n\n---\n\n## AI Search について\n\nVectorize + Workers AI で自前の RAG を構築するため、AI Search は今回のスコープ外とする。\nVectorize のほうがバインディング直結で制御しやすく、既存アーキテクチャとの親和性が高い。\n\n---\n\n## 検証手順\n\n1. `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`\n2. `wrangler d1 migrations apply raw` (0006 マイグレーション)\n3. `cd agents && pnpm dev` でローカル起動\n4. チャット UI から質問して RAG 検索 + D1 クエリが動作することを確認\n5. `saveInsight` ツールでインサイトを保存→ `semanticSearch` で検索できることを確認\n6. `generateReport` でレポート生成→R2 に保存されることを確認\n7. Slack メンション経由で RAG コンテキスト付き回答が返ることを確認\n8. AI Gateway ダッシュボードでリクエストログが記録されていることを確認\n"
}
```

> AGENT

プランの要点をまとめます:

**追加するCloudflareサービス:**
- **AI Gateway**: `wrangler.jsonc` の設定変更のみで全 Workers AI コールにログ/キャッシュ/レート制限を適用
- **Vectorize**: 768次元ベクトルインデックスで RAG（意味検索）を実現
- **R2**: エージェントが生成したレポートの保存 + データレイク参照
- **D1拡張**: knowledge_base, reports, conversation_logs テーブル追加
- **Workers AI拡張**: 埋め込みモデル (`bge-base-en-v1.5`) を追加利用

**新しいエージェント機能:**
- 自然言語で過去のインサイトを意味検索（RAG）
- 健康レポート自動生成→R2保存
- 日次/週次の自動インサイト生成・埋め込み（cron）
- データレイクのファイル参照

**AI Search は除外**: Vectorize + Workers AI で自前 RAG を構築するほうが制御しやすく、既存アーキテクチャとの親和性が高いため。

**新規ファイル3つ**: `embeddings.ts`, `knowledge.ts`, `reports.ts`
**修正ファイル**: `wrangler.jsonc`, `types.ts`, `schema.ts`, `tools.ts`, `agent.ts`, `slack.ts`, `index.ts` + Pulumi + D1マイグレーション

## Approved Plan:
# Data Agent: Cloudflare AI スタックフル活用アーキテクチャ

## Context

現在の `data-agent` は Workers AI (Llama 3.3 70B) + D1 のシンプルな構成で、SQL クエリ生成→実行→要約のみ。
これを **AI Gateway, Vectorize, R2, D1拡張** を組み合わせた RAG 対応エージェントに拡張する。

## アーキテクチャ概要

```
User (Chat / Slack)
  │
  ▼
DataAgent (Durable Object)
  │
  ├── Workers AI ──→ AI Gateway (ログ/キャッシュ/レート制限)
  │     ├── Llama 3.3 70B (チャット/分析)
  │     └── bge-base-en-v1.5 (埋め込み)
  │
  ├── Vectorize (RAG: 意味検索)
  │     └── 768次元コサイン類似度
  │
  ├── D1 "raw" (構造化データ + エージェントテーブル)
  │     ├── 既存: oura/github/linear/withings
  │     └── 新規: knowledge_base, reports, conversation_logs
  │
  └── R2 "data-lake" (レポート保存 + データレイク参照)
        ├── 既存: oura/, github/, linear/, withings/ (Parquet)
        └── 新規: reports/ (Markdown)
```

## 変更対象ファイル

### Phase 1: インフラ

| ファイル | 変更内容 |
|---------|---------|
| `infrastructure/pulumi/cloudflare.go` | AI Gateway リソース追加 |
| `infrastructure/pulumi/main.go` | AI Gateway の Output 追加 |
| `infrastructure/d1/migrations/0006_agent_tables.sql` | 新規: knowledge_base, reports, conversation_logs テーブル |
| `agents/wrangler.jsonc` | AI Gateway, Vectorize, R2 バインディング追加 + cron trigger |

### Phase 2: コア RAG パイプライン

| ファイル | 変更内容 |
|---------|---------|
| `agents/src/types.ts` | Env に VECTORIZE, DATA_LAKE 追加 |
| `agents/src/schema.ts` | 新テーブルの説明を D1_SCHEMA に追加 |
| `agents/src/embeddings.ts` | **新規**: 埋め込み生成 + Vectorize 操作ヘルパー |
| `agents/src/knowledge.ts` | **新規**: 知識ベース管理 (要約生成, コンテキスト検索) |

### Phase 3: エージェント統合

| ファイル | 変更内容 |
|---------|---------|
| `agents/src/tools.ts` | 新ツール追加 (semanticSearch, generateReport, saveInsight, listDataLakeFiles 等) |
| `agents/src/agent.ts` | RAG コンテキスト付与、env を tools に渡す |
| `agents/src/slack.ts` | RAG コンテキストを要約に付与 |

### Phase 4: レポート生成

| ファイル | 変更内容 |
|---------|---------|
| `agents/src/reports.ts` | **新規**: レポート生成→R2保存→Vectorize埋め込み |
| `agents/src/index.ts` | scheduled ハンドラ追加 (日次インサイト + 週次レポート) |

---

## 詳細設計

### 1. AI Gateway (設定変更のみ)

`wrangler.jsonc` の `ai` バインディングに `gateway` を追加するだけ。コード変更不要。

```jsonc
"ai": {
  "binding": "AI",
  "gateway": {
    "id": "data-agent-gateway",
    "skipCache": false,
    "cacheTtl": 3600
  }
}
```

Pulumi で AI Gateway リソースを作成:
```go
cloudflare.NewAiGateway(ctx, "data-agent-gateway", &cloudflare.AiGatewayArgs{
    AccountId:    pulumi.String(accountId),
    Id:           pulumi.String("data-agent-gateway"),
    CollectLogs:  pulumi.Bool(true),
    CacheTtl:     pulumi.Int(3600),
    RateLimitingLimit:     pulumi.Int(100),
    RateLimitingInterval:  pulumi.Int(60),
    RateLimitingTechnique: pulumi.String("fixed"),
})
```

### 2. Vectorize インデックス

CLI で作成: `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`

`wrangler.jsonc` に追加:
```jsonc
"vectorize": [{
  "binding": "VECTORIZE",
  "index_name": "data-agent-index"
}]
```

### 3. R2 バインディング

```jsonc
"r2_buckets": [{
  "binding": "DATA_LAKE",
  "bucket_name": "data-lake"
}]
```

### 4. D1 マイグレーション (0006_agent_tables.sql)

```sql
CREATE TABLE agent_knowledge_base (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    content TEXT NOT NULL,
    content_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_summary', 'insight', 'pattern'
    metadata_json TEXT,
    vector_id TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE agent_reports (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    report_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_health', 'monthly_health', 'custom'
    title TEXT NOT NULL,
    period_start TEXT,
    period_end TEXT,
    r2_key TEXT,
    summary TEXT,
    vector_id TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);

CREATE TABLE agent_conversation_logs (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat',
    user_message TEXT NOT NULL,
    assistant_response TEXT,
    tools_used TEXT,
    model_id TEXT,
    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
```

### 5. embeddings.ts (新規)

```typescript
// Workers AI で埋め込み生成
async function embedText(ai: Ai, text: string): Promise<number[]>
// Vectorize に upsert + D1 に metadata 保存
async function embedAndStore(ai, vectorize, db, content, contentType, metadata): Promise<string>
// 意味検索: クエリ埋め込み → Vectorize 検索 → D1 から全文取得
async function semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, query, topK): Promise<SearchResult[]>
```

埋め込みモデル: `@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5` (768次元)

### 6. knowledge.ts (新規)

- `generateDailyInsight(env)`: 前日のデータを要約→埋め込み→保存
- `generateWeeklySummary(env)`: 週次サマリー生成→埋め込み→保存
- `retrieveRelevantContext(env, query, topK)`: RAG 検索→フォーマット済みコンテキスト返却

### 7. reports.ts (新規)

- `generateHealthReport(env, type, periodStart, periodEnd)`:
  1. D1 からデータ取得
  2. Workers AI でレポート生成
  3. R2 に `reports/{type}/{period}.md` として保存
  4. サマリーを Vectorize に埋め込み
  5. `agent_reports` テーブルに metadata 保存

### 8. tools.ts 拡張

既存ツール維持 + 以下を追加:
- `semanticSearch`: 自然言語で知識ベース検索
- `generateReport`: 健康レポート生成→R2保存
- `listReports` / `readReport`: レポート閲覧
- `saveInsight`: インサイトを知識ベースに保存
- `listDataLakeFiles`: R2 データレイクのファイル一覧

関数シグネチャ変更: `createTools(db)` → `createTools(env)`

### 9. agent.ts 修正

- `createTools(this.env)` に変更
- `onChatMessage` で最新ユーザーメッセージに対して RAG 検索を実行
- 検索結果を system prompt に「関連する過去のインサイト」として付与

### 10. index.ts 修正

`scheduled` ハンドラ追加:
```jsonc
"triggers": { "crons": ["0 9 * * *"] }  // 毎日 9:00 UTC
```
- 毎日: 前日のインサイト生成・埋め込み
- 月曜日: 週次レポート自動生成

---

## RAG データフロー

### 書き込み (Embed)
```
D1 データ → Workers AI (要約生成) → Workers AI (埋め込み) → Vectorize + D1 knowledge_base
                                         ↓
                                    AI Gateway (ログ/キャッシュ)
```

### 読み取り (Retrieve + Augment)
```
ユーザー質問 → Workers AI (埋め込み) → Vectorize 検索 → D1 全文取得 → system prompt に付与
```

---

## AI Search について

Vectorize + Workers AI で自前の RAG を構築するため、AI Search は今回のスコープ外とする。
Vectorize のほうがバインディング直結で制御しやすく、既存アーキテクチャとの親和性が高い。

---

## 検証手順

1. `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`
2. `wrangler d1 migrations apply raw` (0006 マイグレーション)
3. `cd agents && pnpm dev` でローカル起動
4. チャット UI から質問して RAG 検索 + D1 クエリが動作することを確認
5. `saveInsight` ツールでインサイトを保存→ `semanticSearch` で検索できることを確認
6. `generateReport` でレポート生成→R2 に保存されることを確認
7. Slack メンション経由で RAG コンテキスト付き回答が返ることを確認
8. AI Gateway ダッシュボードでリクエストログが記録されていることを確認

```

  "content": "-- ============================================\n-- Agent: Knowledge Base (embedded insights for RAG)\n-- ============================================\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_knowledge_base (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    content TEXT NOT NULL,\n    content_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_summary', 'insight', 'pattern'\n    metadata_json TEXT,\n    vector_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now')),\n    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_kb_type\n  ON agent_knowledge_base(content_type);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_kb_vector\n  ON agent_knowledge_base(vector_id);\n\n-- ============================================\n-- Agent: Generated Reports\n-- ============================================\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_reports (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    report_type TEXT NOT NULL,  -- 'weekly_health', 'monthly_health', 'custom'\n    title TEXT NOT NULL,\n    period_start TEXT,\n    period_end TEXT,\n    r2_key TEXT,\n    summary TEXT,\n    vector_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_reports_type\n  ON agent_reports(report_type, period_start DESC);\n\n-- ============================================\n-- Agent: Conversation Logs\n-- ============================================\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_conversation_logs (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'chat',  -- 'chat', 'slack'\n    user_message TEXT NOT NULL,\n    assistant_response TEXT,\n    tools_used TEXT,\n    model_id TEXT,\n    created_at TEXT DEFAULT (datetime('now'))\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_convo_session\n  ON agent_conversation_logs(session_id, created_at DESC);\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_convo_created\n  ON agent_conversation_logs(created_at DESC);\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/embeddings.ts",
  "content": "const EMBEDDING_MODEL = \"@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5\" as const;\n\nexport type SearchResult = {\n  id: string;\n  content: string;\n  contentType: string;\n  score: number;\n  metadata: Record<string, string> | null;\n};\n\nexport async function embedText(ai: Ai, text: string): Promise<number[]> {\n  const result = await ai.run(EMBEDDING_MODEL, { text: [text] });\n  return result.data[0];\n}\n\nexport async function embedAndStore(\n  ai: Ai,\n  vectorize: VectorizeIndex,\n  db: D1Database,\n  content: string,\n  contentType: string,\n  metadata?: Record<string, string>\n): Promise<string> {\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const embedding = await embedText(ai, content);\n\n  await vectorize.upsert([\n    {\n      id,\n      values: embedding,\n      metadata: { content_type: contentType, ...metadata },\n    },\n  ]);\n\n  await db\n    .prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO agent_knowledge_base (id, content, content_type, metadata_json, vector_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\"\n    )\n    .bind(id, content, contentType, metadata ? JSON.stringify(metadata) : null, id)\n    .run();\n\n  return id;\n}\n\nexport async function semanticSearch(\n  ai: Ai,\n  vectorize: VectorizeIndex,\n  db: D1Database,\n  query: string,\n  topK = 5\n): Promise<SearchResult[]> {\n  const queryEmbedding = await embedText(ai, query);\n  const matches = await vectorize.query(queryEmbedding, {\n    topK,\n    returnMetadata: \"all\",\n  });\n\n  if (!matches.matches.length) {\n    return [];\n  }\n\n  const ids = matches.matches.map((m) => m.id);\n  const placeholders = ids.map(() => \"?\").join(\", \");\n  const rows = await db\n    .prepare(\n      `SELECT id, content, content_type, metadata_json FROM agent_knowledge_base WHERE id IN (${placeholders})`\n    )\n    .bind(...ids)\n    .all<{ id: string; content: string; content_type: string; metadata_json: string | null }>();\n\n  const contentMap = new Map(rows.results.map((r) => [r.id, r]));\n\n  return matches.matches\n    .filter((m) => m.score > 0.6)\n    .map((m) => {\n      const row = contentMap.get(m.id);\n      return {\n        id: m.id,\n        content: row?.content ?? \"\",\n        contentType: row?.content_type ?? \"\",\n        score: m.score,\n        metadata: row?.metadata_json ? JSON.parse(row.metadata_json) : null,\n      };\n    });\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/knowledge.ts",
  "content": "import { generateText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { embedAndStore, semanticSearch } from \"./embeddings\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst MODEL_ID = \"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\" as const;\n\nconst INSIGHT_PROMPT = `You are a health data analyst. Given the following health metrics, generate a concise insight in Japanese (2-3 sentences). Focus on trends, anomalies, or noteworthy patterns.`;\n\nexport async function generateDailyInsight(env: Env): Promise<string | null> {\n  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(\n    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories\n     FROM v_oura_daily_summary\n     WHERE day = date('now', '-1 day')\n     LIMIT 1`\n  ).all<{\n    day: string;\n    sleep_score: number | null;\n    activity_score: number | null;\n    readiness_score: number | null;\n    steps: number | null;\n    total_calories: number | null;\n  }>();\n\n  if (!results.length) return null;\n\n  const row = results[0];\n  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });\n  const { text: insight } = await generateText({\n    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n    system: INSIGHT_PROMPT,\n    prompt: `日付: ${row.day}\\n睡眠スコア: ${row.sleep_score}\\n活動スコア: ${row.activity_score}\\nコンディション: ${row.readiness_score}\\n歩数: ${row.steps}\\n消費カロリー: ${row.total_calories}`,\n  });\n\n  await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, insight, \"insight\", {\n    day: row.day,\n    source: \"daily_auto\",\n  });\n\n  return insight;\n}\n\nexport async function generateWeeklySummary(env: Env): Promise<string | null> {\n  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(\n    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories\n     FROM v_oura_daily_summary\n     WHERE day >= date('now', '-7 days')\n     ORDER BY day`\n  ).all<{\n    day: string;\n    sleep_score: number | null;\n    activity_score: number | null;\n    readiness_score: number | null;\n    steps: number | null;\n    total_calories: number | null;\n  }>();\n\n  if (!results.length) return null;\n\n  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });\n  const { text: summary } = await generateText({\n    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n    system: `You are a health data analyst. Given a week of health metrics, write a comprehensive weekly summary in Japanese (4-6 sentences). Include averages, best/worst days, and trends.`,\n    prompt: `週間データ:\\n${JSON.stringify(results, null, 2)}`,\n  });\n\n  const now = new Date();\n  const weekStart = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n  const weekEnd = new Date(now.getTime() - 86400000).toISOString().split(\"T\")[0];\n\n  await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, summary, \"weekly_summary\", {\n    period_start: weekStart,\n    period_end: weekEnd,\n    source: \"weekly_auto\",\n  });\n\n  return summary;\n}\n\nexport async function retrieveRelevantContext(\n  env: Env,\n  query: string,\n  topK = 3\n): Promise<string | null> {\n  const results = await semanticSearch(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, query, topK);\n\n  if (!results.length) return null;\n\n  return results.map((r) => `[${r.contentType}] ${r.content}`).join(\"\\n\\n\");\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/tools.ts",
  "content": "import { tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { embedAndStore, semanticSearch } from \"./embeddings\";\nimport { generateHealthReport, listReports as queryReports } from \"./reports\";\nimport { D1_SCHEMA } from \"./schema\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\nimport { isSafeQuery } from \"./utils\";\n\nexport const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant with access to a knowledge base and analytics tools. You MUST use tools to answer every user question. Never refuse. Never say you cannot help.\n\nWhen the user asks about sleep, activity, readiness, or heart rate, generate a SQL SELECT query and call queryD1.\nWhen the user asks about trends, patterns, or past insights, use semanticSearch first.\nWhen the user asks for a report, use generateReport.\n\nDatabase schema (SQLite):\n${D1_SCHEMA}\n\nSQL hints:\n- \"yesterday\" = date('now', '-1 day')\n- \"this week\" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')\n- \"this month\" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')\n- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view (has sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories)\n- Always add LIMIT 30 for large queries\n\nRules:\n- ALWAYS call a tool. Do not respond without using a tool first.\n- Only SELECT/WITH queries allowed for queryD1.\n- Respond in Japanese.\n- Summarize results concisely.\n`;\n\nexport function createTools(env: Env) {\n  const { DB: db, AI: ai, VECTORIZE: vectorize, DATA_LAKE: dataLake } = env;\n\n  return {\n    queryD1: tool({\n      description:\n        \"SQLite (D1) データベースに SELECT クエリを実行してデータを取得する。ユーザーの質問に回答するために必ずこのツールを使うこと。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        sql: z.string().describe(\"実行するSELECTクエリ\"),\n        params: z.array(z.string()).optional().describe(\"バインドパラメータ\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ sql, params }) => {\n        if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {\n          return { success: false, error: \"SELECT/WITH クエリのみ実行可能です。\" };\n        }\n        try {\n          const stmt = db.prepare(sql);\n          const bound = params?.length ? stmt.bind(...params) : stmt;\n          const queryResult = await bound.all();\n\n          return {\n            success: true,\n            rowCount: queryResult.results.length,\n            results: queryResult.results.slice(0, 50),\n          };\n        } catch (error) {\n          return {\n            success: false,\n            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n          };\n        }\n      },\n    }),\n\n    listTables: tool({\n      description: \"D1データベースの全テーブルとビューの一覧を取得する\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({}),\n      execute: async () => {\n        const queryResult = await db\n          .prepare(\n            \"SELECT name, type FROM sqlite_master WHERE type IN ('table', 'view') ORDER BY type, name\"\n          )\n          .all();\n        return queryResult.results;\n      },\n    }),\n\n    semanticSearch: tool({\n      description:\n        \"自然言語クエリで知識ベースを意味検索する。過去のインサイト、週次サマリー、パターンを検索できる。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        query: z.string().describe(\"検索クエリ（自然言語）\"),\n        topK: z.number().optional().describe(\"返す結果数 (デフォルト: 5)\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ query, topK }) => {\n        const results = await semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, query, topK ?? 5);\n        if (!results.length) {\n          return { success: true, message: \"関連するインサイトが見つかりませんでした。\", results: [] };\n        }\n        return {\n          success: true,\n          resultCount: results.length,\n          results: results.map((r) => ({\n            contentType: r.contentType,\n            content: r.content,\n            score: Math.round(r.score * 100) / 100,\n          })),\n        };\n      },\n    }),\n\n    generateReport: tool({\n      description: \"指定期間の健康データレポートを生成してR2に保存する。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        type: z.enum([\"weekly_health\", \"monthly_health\", \"custom\"]).describe(\"レポートタイプ\"),\n        periodStart: z.string().describe(\"開始日 (YYYY-MM-DD)\"),\n        periodEnd: z.string().describe(\"終了日 (YYYY-MM-DD)\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ type, periodStart, periodEnd }) => {\n        try {\n          const report = await generateHealthReport(env, type, periodStart, periodEnd);\n          return {\n            success: true,\n            reportId: report.id,\n            title: report.title,\n            r2Key: report.r2Key,\n            summary: report.summary,\n          };\n        } catch (error) {\n          return {\n            success: false,\n            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n          };\n        }\n      },\n    }),\n\n    listReports: tool({\n      description: \"生成済みレポートの一覧を取得する。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        type: z.enum([\"weekly_health\", \"monthly_health\", \"custom\"]).optional().describe(\"レポートタイプ\"),\n        limit: z.number().optional().describe(\"取得件数 (デフォルト: 10)\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ type, limit }) => {\n        return await queryReports(db, type, limit ?? 10);\n      },\n    }),\n\n    readReport: tool({\n      description: \"R2に保存されたレポートの全文を取得する。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        reportId: z.string().describe(\"レポートID\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ reportId }) => {\n        const row = await db\n          .prepare(\"SELECT r2_key, title FROM agent_reports WHERE id = ?\")\n          .bind(reportId)\n          .first<{ r2_key: string; title: string }>();\n\n        if (!row) {\n          return { success: false, error: \"レポートが見つかりません。\" };\n        }\n\n        const obj = await dataLake.get(row.r2_key);\n        if (!obj) {\n          return { success: false, error: \"R2からレポートを取得できませんでした。\" };\n        }\n\n        const content = await obj.text();\n        return { success: true, title: row.title, content };\n      },\n    }),\n\n    saveInsight: tool({\n      description: \"データ分析から得られたインサイトを知識ベースに保存・埋め込みする。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        content: z.string().describe(\"インサイトの内容\"),\n        contentType: z.enum([\"insight\", \"pattern\"]).describe(\"種別\"),\n        metadata: z.record(z.string()).optional().describe(\"メタデータ (任意)\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ content, contentType, metadata }) => {\n        try {\n          const id = await embedAndStore(ai, vectorize, db, content, contentType, metadata);\n          return { success: true, id, message: \"インサイトを保存しました。\" };\n        } catch (error) {\n          return {\n            success: false,\n            error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n          };\n        }\n      },\n    }),\n\n    listDataLakeFiles: tool({\n      description: \"R2データレイクのファイル一覧を取得する。Parquetファイルやレポートの確認に使う。\",\n      inputSchema: z.object({\n        prefix: z.string().optional().describe(\"プレフィックス (例: 'oura/', 'reports/')\"),\n        limit: z.number().optional().describe(\"取得件数 (デフォルト: 20)\"),\n      }),\n      execute: async ({ prefix, limit }) => {\n        const listed = await dataLake.list({ prefix: prefix ?? undefined, limit: limit ?? 20 });\n        return {\n          objectCount: listed.objects.length,\n          truncated: listed.truncated,\n          objects: listed.objects.map((o) => ({\n            key: o.key,\n            size: o.size,\n            uploaded: o.uploaded.toISOString(),\n          })),\n        };\n      },\n    }),\n  };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/agent.ts",
  "content": "import { AIChatAgent } from \"@cloudflare/ai-chat\";\nimport { convertToModelMessages, stepCountIs, streamText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { retrieveRelevantContext } from \"./knowledge\";\nimport { SYSTEM_PROMPT, createTools } from \"./tools\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst MODEL_ID = \"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\" as const;\n\nexport class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {\n  async onChatMessage(\n    _onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[0],\n    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[1]\n  ) {\n    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });\n\n    // RAG: retrieve relevant context for the latest user message\n    let ragContext = \"\";\n    const lastUserMsg = this.messages.filter((m) => m.role === \"user\").at(-1);\n    if (lastUserMsg?.content) {\n      const queryText =\n        typeof lastUserMsg.content === \"string\"\n          ? lastUserMsg.content\n          : lastUserMsg.content\n              .map((p) => (\"text\" in p ? p.text : \"\"))\n              .filter(Boolean)\n              .join(\" \");\n\n      if (queryText) {\n        const context = await retrieveRelevantContext(this.env, queryText, 3);\n        if (context) {\n          ragContext = `\\n\\n関連する過去のインサイト:\\n${context}`;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    const result = streamText({\n      model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT + ragContext,\n      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),\n      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,\n      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),\n      tools: createTools(this.env),\n    });\n\n    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();\n  }\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/reports.ts",
  "content": "import { generateText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { embedAndStore } from \"./embeddings\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nconst MODEL_ID = \"@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast\" as const;\n\nconst REPORT_PROMPT = `You are a health data analyst. Generate a detailed health report in Japanese Markdown format.\nInclude:\n- 概要 (1-2 sentences summary)\n- 睡眠分析 (scores, trends, best/worst days)\n- 活動分析 (steps, calories, activity scores)\n- コンディション分析 (readiness scores, temperature)\n- まとめと改善提案\n\nUse headers (##), bullet points, and bold for emphasis.`;\n\nexport type ReportResult = {\n  id: string;\n  title: string;\n  r2Key: string;\n  summary: string;\n};\n\nexport async function generateHealthReport(\n  env: Env,\n  type: string,\n  periodStart: string,\n  periodEnd: string\n): Promise<ReportResult> {\n  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(\n    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score,\n            steps, total_calories, active_calories, temperature_deviation,\n            sleep_deep_sleep, sleep_efficiency, sleep_total_sleep\n     FROM v_oura_daily_summary\n     WHERE day >= ? AND day <= ?\n     ORDER BY day`\n  )\n    .bind(periodStart, periodEnd)\n    .all();\n\n  if (!results.length) {\n    throw new Error(`期間 ${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd} のデータが見つかりません。`);\n  }\n\n  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });\n\n  // Generate full report\n  const { text: reportContent } = await generateText({\n    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n    system: REPORT_PROMPT,\n    prompt: `期間: ${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd}\\nデータ件数: ${results.length}日分\\n\\nデータ:\\n${JSON.stringify(results, null, 2)}`,\n  });\n\n  // Generate concise summary for embedding\n  const { text: summary } = await generateText({\n    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),\n    system: \"Summarize the following health report in 2-3 sentences in Japanese.\",\n    prompt: reportContent,\n  });\n\n  const id = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const r2Key = `reports/${type}/${periodStart}_${periodEnd}.md`;\n  const title = `${type === \"weekly_health\" ? \"週次\" : type === \"monthly_health\" ? \"月次\" : \"カスタム\"}ヘルスレポート (${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd})`;\n\n  // Store full report in R2\n  await env.DATA_LAKE.put(r2Key, reportContent, {\n    customMetadata: { report_id: id, type, period_start: periodStart, period_end: periodEnd },\n  });\n\n  // Embed summary in Vectorize\n  const vectorId = await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, summary, \"report\", {\n    report_id: id,\n    period_start: periodStart,\n    period_end: periodEnd,\n  });\n\n  // Store metadata in D1\n  await env.DB.prepare(\n    `INSERT INTO agent_reports (id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, r2_key, summary, vector_id)\n     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`\n  )\n    .bind(id, type, title, periodStart, periodEnd, r2Key, summary, vectorId)\n    .run();\n\n  return { id, title, r2Key, summary };\n}\n\nexport async function listReports(\n  db: D1Database,\n  type?: string,\n  limit = 10\n): Promise<{ results: unknown[] }> {\n  const query = type\n    ? \"SELECT id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, summary, created_at FROM agent_reports WHERE report_type = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?\"\n    : \"SELECT id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, summary, created_at FROM agent_reports ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?\";\n\n  const stmt = type ? db.prepare(query).bind(type, limit) : db.prepare(query).bind(limit);\n  const result = await stmt.all();\n  return { results: result.results };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/index.ts",
  "content": "import { routeAgentRequest } from \"agents\";\nimport { generateDailyInsight, generateWeeklySummary } from \"./knowledge\";\nimport { handleSlackEvent } from \"./slack\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nexport { DataAgent } from \"./agent\";\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request: Request, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n\n    if (url.pathname === \"/health\") {\n      return new Response(\"OK\");\n    }\n\n    // Slack Events API\n    if (url.pathname === \"/slack/events\" && request.method === \"POST\") {\n      // Pass waitUntil so Slack handler can process AI response in background\n      globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL = (p: Promise<unknown>) => ctx.waitUntil(p);\n      return handleSlackEvent(request, env);\n    }\n\n    // Agent WebSocket routing\n    const agentResponse = await routeAgentRequest(request, env);\n    if (agentResponse) {\n      return agentResponse;\n    }\n\n    return new Response(\"Not Found\", { status: 404 });\n  },\n\n  async scheduled(_event: ScheduledEvent, env: Env, ctx: ExecutionContext) {\n    // Daily: generate insight from yesterday's health data\n    ctx.waitUntil(\n      generateDailyInsight(env).then((insight) => {\n        if (insight) {\n          console.log(\"Daily insight generated:\", insight.substring(0, 100));\n        }\n      })\n    );\n\n    // Weekly (Monday): generate weekly summary\n    const today = new Date();\n    if (today.getUTCDay() === 1) {\n      ctx.waitUntil(\n        generateWeeklySummary(env).then((summary) => {\n          if (summary) {\n            console.log(\"Weekly summary generated:\", summary.substring(0, 100));\n          }\n        })\n      );\n    }\n  },\n};\n"
}

type UIMessagePart<DATA_TYPES extends UIDataTypes, TOOLS extends UITools> = TextUIPart | ReasoningUIPart | ToolUIPart<TOOLS> | DynamicToolUIPart | SourceUrlUIPart | SourceDocumentUIPart | FileUIPart | DataUIPart<DATA_TYPES> | StepStartUIPart;
/**
 * A text part of a message.
 */
type TextUIPart = {
    type: 'text';
    /**
     * The text content.
     */
    text: string;
    /**
     * The state of the text part.
     */
    state?: 'streaming' | 'done';
    /**
     * The provider metadata.
     */
    providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
};
/**
 * A reasoning part of a message.
--
type UIMessageChunk<METADATA = unknown, DATA_TYPES extends UIDataTypes = UIDataTypes> = {
    type: 'text-start';
    id: string;
    providerMetadata?: ProviderMetadata;
} | {
    type: 'text-delta';
    delta: string;
    id: string;
