interface UIMessage<METADATA = unknown, DATA_PARTS extends UIDataTypes = UIDataTypes, TOOLS extends UITools = UITools> {
    /**
     * A unique identifier for the message.
     */
    id: string;
    /**
     * The role of the message.
     */
    role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant';
    /**
     * The metadata of the message.
     */
    metadata?: METADATA;
    /**
     * The parts of the message. Use this for rendering the message in the UI.
     *
     * System messages should be avoided (set the system prompt on the server instead).
     * They can have text parts.
     *
     * User messages can have text parts and file parts.
     *
     * Assistant messages can have text, reasoning, tool invocation, and file parts.
     */
    parts: Array<UIMessagePart<DATA_PARTS, TOOLS>>;
}
type UIMessagePart<DATA_TYPES extends UIDataTypes, TOOLS extends UITools> = TextUIPart | ReasoningUIPart | ToolUIPart<TOOLS> | DynamicToolUIPart | SourceUrlUIPart | SourceDocumentUIPart | FileUIPart | DataUIPart<DATA_TYPES> | StepStartUIPart;

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/utils.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, expect, it } from \"vitest\";\nimport { isSafeQuery } from \"./utils\";\n\ndescribe(\"isSafeQuery\", () => {\n  describe(\"許可されるクエリ\", () => {\n    it(\"SELECTクエリを許可する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"SELECT * FROM users\")).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it(\"WITHクエリ (CTE) を許可する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"WITH cte AS (SELECT 1) SELECT * FROM cte\")).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it(\"末尾にセミコロンがあるSELECTクエリを許可する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"SELECT 1;\")).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it(\"小文字のselectを許可する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"select day, score from oura_daily_sleep\")).toBe(true);\n    });\n\n    it(\"前後に空白があるクエリを許可する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"  SELECT 1  \")).toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe(\"拒否されるクエリ\", () => {\n    it(\"INSERTクエリを拒否する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"INSERT INTO users VALUES (1)\")).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"UPDATEクエリを拒否する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"UPDATE users SET name = 'x'\")).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"DELETEクエリを拒否する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"DELETE FROM users\")).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"DROP TABLEを拒否する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"DROP TABLE users\")).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"複数ステートメント (SQLインジェクション) を拒否する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"SELECT 1; DROP TABLE users\")).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it(\"空文字列を拒否する\", () => {\n      expect(isSafeQuery(\"\")).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n});\n"
}

{

  "content": "import { type Mock, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from \"vitest\";\nimport { embedAndStore, embedText, semanticSearch } from \"./embeddings\";\n\n// --- テストヘルパー: モック生成 ---\n\nfunction createMockAi(embedding: number[] = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3]): Ai {\n  return {\n    run: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ data: [embedding] }),\n  } as unknown as Ai;\n}\n\nfunction createMockVectorize(matches: Array<{ id: string; score: number }> = []): VectorizeIndex {\n  return {\n    upsert: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined),\n    query: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ matches }),\n  } as unknown as VectorizeIndex;\n}\n\nfunction createMockDb(\n  results: unknown[] = []\n): D1Database & { _prepareArgs: string[]; _bindArgs: unknown[][] } {\n  const bindArgs: unknown[][] = [];\n  const prepareArgs: string[] = [];\n  const mockStmt = {\n    bind: vi.fn((...args: unknown[]) => {\n      bindArgs.push(args);\n      return mockStmt;\n    }),\n    run: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ success: true }),\n    all: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue({ results }),\n  };\n  return {\n    prepare: vi.fn((sql: string) => {\n      prepareArgs.push(sql);\n      return mockStmt;\n    }),\n    _prepareArgs: prepareArgs,\n    _bindArgs: bindArgs,\n  } as unknown as D1Database & { _prepareArgs: string[]; _bindArgs: unknown[][] };\n}\n\n// --- テスト本体 ---\n\ndescribe(\"embedText\", () => {\n  it(\"Workers AIの埋め込みモデルを呼び出して結果を返す\", async () => {\n    const expectedEmbedding = [0.1, 0.2, 0.3];\n    const ai = createMockAi(expectedEmbedding);\n\n    const result = await embedText(ai, \"テストテキスト\");\n\n    expect(result).toEqual(expectedEmbedding);\n    expect(ai.run).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\"@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5\", {\n      text: [\"テストテキスト\"],\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"空文字列でも呼び出し可能\", async () => {\n    const ai = createMockAi([0, 0, 0]);\n\n    const result = await embedText(ai, \"\");\n\n    expect(result).toEqual([0, 0, 0]);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"embedAndStore\", () => {\n  let ai: Ai;\n  let vectorize: VectorizeIndex;\n  let db: ReturnType<typeof createMockDb>;\n\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    ai = createMockAi([0.5, 0.6, 0.7]);\n    vectorize = createMockVectorize();\n    db = createMockDb();\n    vi.stubGlobal(\"crypto\", {\n      randomUUID: () => \"test-uuid-1234\",\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"埋め込みを生成してVectorizeに保存し、D1にメタデータを記録する\", async () => {\n    const id = await embedAndStore(ai, vectorize, db, \"インサイト内容\", \"insight\", {\n      day: \"2026-04-03\",\n    });\n\n    // UUIDを返す\n    expect(id).toBe(\"test-uuid-1234\");\n\n    // Vectorizeにupsertされる\n    expect(vectorize.upsert).toHaveBeenCalledWith([\n      {\n        id: \"test-uuid-1234\",\n        values: [0.5, 0.6, 0.7],\n        metadata: { content_type: \"insight\", day: \"2026-04-03\" },\n      },\n    ]);\n\n    // D1にINSERTされる\n    expect(db._prepareArgs[0]).toContain(\"INSERT INTO agent_knowledge_base\");\n    expect(db._bindArgs[0]).toEqual([\n      \"test-uuid-1234\",\n      \"インサイト内容\",\n      \"insight\",\n      '{\"day\":\"2026-04-03\"}',\n      \"test-uuid-1234\",\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  it(\"metadataがundefinedの場合、D1にはnullが保存される\", async () => {\n    await embedAndStore(ai, vectorize, db, \"content\", \"pattern\");\n\n    expect(db._bindArgs[0][3]).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"semanticSearch\", () => {\n  it(\"Vectorize検索結果をD1と結合して返す\", async () => {\n    const ai = createMockAi([0.1, 0.2, 0.3]);\n    const vectorize = createMockVectorize([\n      { id: \"vec-1\", score: 0.95 },\n      { id: \"vec-2\", score: 0.80 },\n    ]);\n    const db = createMockDb([\n      { id: \"vec-1\", content: \"高スコアの結果\", content_type: \"insight\", metadata_json: null },\n      {\n        id: \"vec-2\",\n        content: \"中スコアの結果\",\n        content_type: \"weekly_summary\",\n        metadata_json: '{\"period\":\"2026-W14\"}',\n      },\n    ]);\n\n    const results = await semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, \"検索クエリ\", 5);\n\n    expect(results).toHaveLength(2);\n    expect(results[0]).toEqual({\n      id: \"vec-1\",\n      content: \"高スコアの結果\",\n      contentType: \"insight\",\n      score: 0.95,\n      metadata: null,\n    });\n    expect(results[1]).toEqual({\n      id: \"vec-2\",\n      content: \"中スコアの結果\",\n      contentType: \"weekly_summary\",\n      score: 0.80,\n      metadata: { period: \"2026-W14\" },\n    });\n  });\n\n  it(\"スコアが0.6以下の結果をフィルタリングする\", async () => {\n    const ai = createMockAi();\n    const vectorize = createMockVectorize([\n      { id: \"high\", score: 0.9 },\n      { id: \"low\", score: 0.5 },\n    ]);\n    const db = createMockDb([\n      { id: \"high\", content: \"関連あり\", content_type: \"insight\", metadata_json: null },\n      { id: \"low\", content: \"関連薄い\", content_type: \"insight\", metadata_json: null },\n    ]);\n\n    const results = await semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, \"query\");\n\n    expect(results).toHaveLength(1);\n    expect(results[0].id).toBe(\"high\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"Vectorizeから結果がない場合、空配列を返す\", async () => {\n    const ai = createMockAi();\n    const vectorize = createMockVectorize([]);\n    const db = createMockDb();\n\n    const results = await semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, \"存在しないトピック\");\n\n    expect(results).toEqual([]);\n    // D1は呼ばれない\n    expect(db.prepare).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it(\"topKパラメータをVectorizeに渡す\", async () => {\n    const ai = createMockAi();\n    const vectorize = createMockVectorize([]);\n    const db = createMockDb();\n\n    await semanticSearch(ai, vectorize, db, \"query\", 3);\n\n    expect(vectorize.query).toHaveBeenCalledWith([0.1, 0.2, 0.3], {\n      topK: 3,\n      returnMetadata: \"all\",\n    });\n  });\n});\n"
}

readiness_score: 90,\n        steps: 8500,\n        total_calories: 2200,\n      },\n    ]);\n\n    const result = await generateDailyInsight(env);\n\n    expect(result).toBe(\"生成されたインサイト\");\n expect(generateText).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();\n    expect(embedAndStore).toHaveBeenCalledWith(\n      env.AI,\n      env.VECTORIZE,\n      env.DB,\n      \"生成されたインサイト\",\n      \"weekly_summary\",\n      expect.objectContaining({ source: \"weekly_auto\" })\n    );\n  });\n\n  it(\"データがない場合、nullを返す\", async () => {\n    const env = createMockEnv([]);\n\n    const result = await generateWeeklySummary(env);\n\n    expect(result).toBeNull();\n    expect(generateText).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"retrieveRelevantContext\", () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.clearAllMocks();\n  });\n\n  it(\"検索結果がある場合、フォーマットされたコンテキスト文字列を返す\", async () => {\n    (semanticSearch as Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce([\n      { id: \"1\", content: \"先週の睡眠は良好\", contentType: \"weekly_summary\", score: 0.9, metadata: null },\n      { id: \"2\", content: \"運動量と睡眠の相関\", contentType: \"insight\", score: 0.8, metadata: null },\n    ]);\n    const env = createMockEnv();\n\n    const result = await retrieveRelevantContext(env, \"睡眠の傾向は？\", 3);\n\n    expect(result).toBe(\n      \"[weekly_summary] 先週の睡眠は良好\\n\\n[insight] 運動量と睡眠の相関\"\n    );\n    expect(semanticSearch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, \"睡眠の傾向は？\", 3);\n  });\n\n  it(\"検索結果がない場合、nullを返す\", async () => {\n    (semanticSearch as Mock).mockResolvedValueOnce([]);\n    const env = createMockEnv();\n\n    const result = await retrieveRelevantContext(env, \"存在しないトピック\");\n\n    expect(result).toBeNull();\n  });\n});\n"
}

---

## 実装サマリー

### 新サービス統合

| サービス | 用途 | 設定 |
|---------|------|------|
| **AI Gateway** | 全AI呼び出しのログ/キャッシュ/レート制限 | `wrangler.jsonc` + Pulumi |
| **Vectorize** | RAG用768次元ベクトルインデックス | `wrangler.jsonc` バインディング |
| **R2** | レポート保存 + データレイク参照 | `wrangler.jsonc` バインディング |
| **D1拡張** | knowledge_base / reports / conversation_logs | マイグレーション0006 |
| **Workers AI** | 埋め込みモデル `bge-base-en-v1.5` 追加 | AI Gateway経由 |

### 新規ファイル (4)
- `agents/src/embeddings.ts` - 埋め込み生成 + Vectorize操作
- `agents/src/knowledge.ts` - 知識ベース管理 (日次/週次インサイト生成)
- `agents/src/reports.ts` - レポート生成→R2保存→埋め込み
- `infrastructure/d1/migrations/0006_agent_tables.sql`

### 新ツール (7)
`semanticSearch`, `generateReport`, `listReports`, `readReport`, `saveInsight`, `listDataLakeFiles` + 既存2つ維持

### テスト (30テスト)
- `utils.test.ts` (11) - SQLバリデーション境界値
- `embeddings.test.ts` (8) - 埋め込み/保存/検索パイプライン
- `knowledge.test.ts` (6) - インサイト生成/コンテキスト取得
- `reports.test.ts` (5) - レポート生成/一覧

### デプロイ前に必要な手順
1. `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`
2. `wrangler d1 migrations apply raw`
3. Pulumi で AI Gateway リソースを作成 (`pulumi up`)

---

ご質問の「もう使える実装になっている？」についてですが、**コードは完成していますが、デプロイ前に3つの手順が必要です**:

1. **Vectorize インデックス作成**: `wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=768 --metric=cosine`
2. **D1 マイグレーション適用**: `wrangler d1 migrations apply raw`
3. **Pulumi 適用**: `pulumi up` (AI Gateway リソース作成)

これらを実行すれば `wrangler dev` でローカル動作確認、`wrangler deploy` で本番デプロイできます。

> DEVELOPER

どんなアーキテクチャ？

> AGENT

こんな構成です:

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  User Interface                                     │
│  ├── Chat UI (WebSocket)                            │
│  └── Slack (app_mention)                            │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
               │
               ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DataAgent (Durable Object)                         │
│  ├── 会話ステート永続化 (SQLite on DO)                │
│  └── ツール実行 (8種)                                │
└──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
               │
      ┌────────┼────────┬────────────┐
      ▼        ▼        ▼            ▼
┌──────────┐ ┌─────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌─────┐
│Workers AI│ │ D1  │ │Vectorize │ │ R2  │
│(via GW)  │ │"raw"│ │768d cos  │ │data │
└────┬─────┘ └──┬──┘ └────┬─────┘ │lake │
     │          │          │       └──┬──┘
     ▼          │          │          │
┌──────────┐   │          │          │
│AI Gateway│   │          │          │
│• ログ    │   │          │          │
│• キャッシュ│   │          │          │
│• レート制限│   │          │          │
└──────────┘   │          │          │
               │          │          │
     ┌─────────┴──────────┴──────────┘
     ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  データフロー                         │
│                                     │
│  【RAG 読み取り】チャット時           │
│  質問 → bge埋め込み → Vectorize検索  │
│       → D1から全文取得               │
│       → system promptに付与         │
│       → Llama 3.3で回答生成         │
│                                     │
│  【RAG 書き込み】Cron毎日9時UTC      │
│  D1集計 → Llama 3.3で要約生成       │
│         → bge埋め込み               │
│         → Vectorize + D1に保存      │
│                                     │
│  【レポート】ツール or Cron(月曜)    │
│  D1データ → Llama 3.3でMD生成       │
│           → R2に保存                │
│           → サマリーをVectorizeに    │
└─────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 各サービスの役割

| サービス | 役割 |
|---------|------|
| **AI Gateway** | Workers AI の全リクエストを透過的にプロキシ。ログ収集、同一プロンプトのキャッシュ(1h)、100req/min制限 |
| **Workers AI** | Llama 3.3 70B (チャット/SQL生成/レポート) + bge-base-en-v1.5 (埋め込み768次元) |
| **Vectorize** | 健康インサイト・週次サマリー・レポート要約のベクトルを格納。コサイン類似度で意味検索 |
| **D1** | 既存の健康データ(Oura/GitHub/Linear/Withings) + 新テーブル3つ(knowledge_base, reports, conversation_logs) |
| **R2** | 生成レポートをMarkdownで保存(`reports/weekly_health/2026-03-28_2026-04-03.md`等) |
| **Durable Objects** | エージェントの会話状態・WebSocket管理 |

### エージェントのツール一覧

| ツール | 用途 |
|-------|------|
| `queryD1` | SQLでD1を直接クエリ |
| `listTables` | テーブル/ビュー一覧 |
| `semanticSearch` | 自然言語で知識ベースをRAG検索 |
| `generateReport` | 期間指定で健康レポート生成→R2保存 |
| `listReports` / `readReport` | 生成済みレポートの閲覧 |
| `saveInsight` | 会話中に発見したパターンを埋め込み保存 |
| `listDataLakeFiles` | R2のParquetファイル等を一覧 |

ポイントは **AI Gateway が設定変更だけで全AI呼び出しに効く**ことと、**Vectorize + D1 の2層構成**（ベクトルはVectorize、全文はD1）で RAG を実現している点です。

> DEVELOPER

AI　Gatewayはまだ1つのモデルだけになっている？

> AGENT

いいえ、AI Gateway 自体は **2つのモデル**を通しています:

- **Llama 3.3 70B** (チャット/SQL生成/レポート)
- **bge-base-en-v1.5** (埋め込み)

`wrangler.jsonc` で `ai` バインディングに `gateway` を設定しているので、`env.AI` 経由の全呼び出しが自動的にゲートウェイを通ります。

ただし、**フォールバック（代替モデルへの自動切替）は未設定**です。AI Gateway にはリクエスト失敗時に別モデルに自動フォールバックする機能がありますが、今はそれを使っていません。

例えば「70Bが応答しなければ8Bにフォールバック」のような設定を追加しますか？あるいは、用途別にモデルを分けたい（SQL生成は軽量モデル、分析は大型モデル等）という意図でしょうか？

> DEVELOPER

この二つは無料？

# Pricing

Note

Workers AI has updated pricing to be more granular, with per-model unit-based pricing presented, but still billing in neurons in the back end.

# Pricing

Note

Workers AI has updated pricing to be more granular, with per-model unit-based pricing presented, but still billing in neurons in the back end.

Workers AI is included in both the [Free and Paid Workers plans](/workers/platform/pricing/) and is priced at **$0.011 per 1,000 Neurons**.

Our free allocation allows anyone to use a total of **10,000 Neurons per day at no charge**. To use more than 10,000 Neurons per day, you need to sign up for the [Workers Paid plan](/workers/platform/pricing/#workers). On Workers Paid, you will be charged at $0.011 / 1,000 Neurons for any usage above the free allocation of 10,000 Neurons per day.

You can monitor your Neuron usage in the [Cloudflare Workers AI dashboard ↗](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/ai/workers-ai).

All limits reset daily at 00:00 UTC. If you exceed any one of the above limits, further operations will fail with an error.

| Free  allocation | Pricing                |                               |
| ---------------- | ---------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Workers Free     | 10,000 Neurons per day | N/A - Upgrade to Workers Paid |
| Workers Paid     | 10,000 Neurons per day | $0.011 / 1,000 Neurons        |

## What are Neurons?

Neurons are our way of measuring AI outputs across different models, representing the GPU compute needed to perform your request. Our serverless model allows you to pay only for what you use without having to worry about renting, managing, or scaling GPUs.

Note

The Price in Tokens column is equivalent to the Price in Neurons column - the different units are displayed so you can easily compare and understand pricing.

title: Workers AI updated pricing

[Skip to content](#%5Ftop) 

# Changelog

New updates and improvements at Cloudflare.

[ Subscribe to RSS ](/changelog/rss/index.xml) [ View RSS feeds ](/fundamentals/new-features/available-rss-feeds/) 

![hero image](/_astro/hero.CVYJHPAd_26AMqX.svg) 

[ ← Back to all posts ](/changelog/) 

## Workers AI updated pricing

Feb 20, 2025 

[ Workers AI ](/workers-ai/) 

We've updated the Workers AI [pricing](/workers-ai/platform/pricing/) to include the latest models and how model usage maps to Neurons.

* Each model's core input format(s) (tokens, audio seconds, images, etc) now include mappings to Neurons, making it easier to understand how your included Neuron volume is consumed and how you are charged at scale
* Per-model pricing, instead of the previous bucket approach, allows us to be more flexible on how models are charged based on their size, performance and capabilities. As we optimize each model, we can then pass on savings for that model.
* You will still only pay for what you consume: Workers AI inference is serverless, and not billed by the hour.

Going forward, models will be launched with their associated Neuron costs, and we'll be updating the Workers AI dashboard and API to reflect consumption in both raw units and Neurons. Visit the [Workers AI pricing](/workers-ai/platform/pricing/) page to learn more about Workers AI pricing.
</text>
</result>
```

> AGENT

**無料ではありません**。どちらも Neuron を消費します。

### 料金体系

| モデル | 料金 | Neuron 消費 |
|-------|------|------------|
| **bge-base-en-v1.5** (埋め込み) | $0.067 / 100万 input tokens | 6,058 neurons / 100万 tokens |
| **Llama 3.3 70B** (チャット) | ドキュメントに明示なし（70Bクラスは高め） | 大量消費 |

### 無料枠

- **10,000 Neurons/日** が Free / Paid どちらでも無料
- 超過分は **$0.011 / 1,000 Neurons** (Workers Paid プランが必要)

### 現実的なコスト感

70B モデルは 1 リクエストあたりの Neuron 消費が大きく、無料枠だと **数回の会話で使い切る可能性が高い** です。

### コスト最適化案

もし無料枠内で運用したいなら:

| 用途 | 現在 | 代替 (軽量) | 効果 |
|------|------|------------|------|
| チャット/分析 | Llama 3.3 **70B** | `@cf/qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8` ($0.051/M input) | Neuron 大幅削減 |
| SQL生成 (Slack) | Llama 3.3 70B | `@cf/meta/llama-4-scout-17b-16e-instruct` ($0.27/M input) | 中程度の削減 |
| 埋め込み | bge-**base** | `@cf/baai/bge-m3` ($0.012/M input) | 約1/6のコスト |

モデルを変更しますか？AI Gateway のキャッシュ (TTL 1h) が効くので同じ質問は再課金されませんが、70B は根本的にコストが高いです。

> DEVELOPER

> AGENT

10	export class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {
11	  async onChatMessage(
12	    _onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>["onChatMessage"]>[0],
13	    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>["onChatMessage"]>[1]
14	  ) {
15	    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });
16	
17	    // RAG: retrieve relevant context for the latest user message
18	    let ragContext = "";
19	    const lastUserMsg = this.messages.filter((m) => m.role === "user").at(-1);
20	    if (lastUserMsg?.parts) {
21	      const queryText = lastUserMsg.parts
22	        .filter((p): p is { type: "text"; text: string } => p.type === "text")
23	        .map((p) => p.text)
24	        .join(" ");
25	
26	      if (queryText) {
27	        const context = await retrieveRelevantContext(this.env, queryText, 3);
28	        if (context) {
29	          ragContext = `\n\n関連する過去のインサイト:\n${context}`;
30	        }
31	      }
32	    }
33	
34	    const result = streamText({
35	      model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
36	      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT + ragContext,
37	      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),
38	      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,
39	      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),
40	      tools: createTools(this.env),
41	    });
42	
43	    return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();
44	  }
45	}
46

1	const EMBEDDING_MODEL = "@cf/baai/bge-base-en-v1.5" as const;
2	
3	export type SearchResult = {
4	  id: string;
5	  content: string;
6	  contentType: string;
7	  score: number;
8	  metadata: Record<string, string> | null;
9	};
10	
11	export async function embedText(ai: Ai, text: string): Promise<number[]> {
12	  const result = (await ai.run(EMBEDDING_MODEL, { text: [text] })) as { data: number[][] };
13	  return result.data[0];
14	}
15	
16	export async function embedAndStore(
17	  ai: Ai,
18	  vectorize: VectorizeIndex,
19	  db: D1Database,
20	  content: string,
21	  contentType: string,
22	  metadata?: Record<string, string>
23	): Promise<string> {
24	  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
25	  const embedding = await embedText(ai, content);
26	
27	  await vectorize.upsert([
28	    {
29	      id,
30	      values: embedding,
31	      metadata: { content_type: contentType, ...metadata },
32	    },
33	  ]);
34	
35	  await db
36	    .prepare(
37	      "INSERT INTO agent_knowledge_base (id, content, content_type, metadata_json, vector_id) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)"
38	    )
39	    .bind(id, content, contentType, metadata ? JSON.stringify(metadata) : null, id)
40	    .run();
41	
42	  return id;
43	}
44	
45	export async function semanticSearch(
46	  ai: Ai,
47	  vectorize: VectorizeIndex,
48	  db: D1Database,
49	  query: string,
50	  topK = 5
51	): Promise<SearchResult[]> {
52	  const queryEmbedding = await embedText(ai, query);
53	  const matches = await vectorize.query(queryEmbedding, {
54	    topK,
55	    returnMetadata: "all",
56	  });
57	
58	  if (!matches.matches.length) {
59	    return [];
60	  }
61	
62	  const ids = matches.matches.map((m) => m.id);
63	  const placeholders = ids.map(() => "?").join(", ");
64	  const rows = await db
65	    .prepare(
66	      `SELECT id, content, content_type, metadata_json FROM agent_knowledge_base WHERE id IN (${placeholders})`
67	    )
68	    .bind(...ids)
69	    .all<{ id: string; content: string; content_type: string; metadata_json: string | null }>();
70	
71	  const contentMap = new Map(rows.results.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
72	
73	  return matches.matches
74	    .filter((m) => m.score > 0.6)
75	    .map((m) => {
76	      const row = contentMap.get(m.id);
77	      return {
78	        id: m.id,
79	        content: row?.content ?? "",
80	        contentType: row?.content_type ?? "",
81	        score: m.score,
82	        metadata: row?.metadata_json ? JSON.parse(row.metadata_json) : null,
83	      };
84	    });
85	}
86

1	import { generateText } from "ai";
2	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
3	import { embedAndStore, semanticSearch } from "./embeddings";
4	import type { Env } from "./types";
5	
6	const MODEL_ID = "@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast" as const;
7	
8	const INSIGHT_PROMPT = `You are a health data analyst. Given the following health metrics, generate a concise insight in Japanese (2-3 sentences). Focus on trends, anomalies, or noteworthy patterns.`;
9	
10	export async function generateDailyInsight(env: Env): Promise<string | null> {
11	  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
12	    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories
13	     FROM v_oura_daily_summary
14	     WHERE day = date('now', '-1 day')
15	     LIMIT 1`
16	  ).all<{
17	    day: string;
18	    sleep_score: number | null;
19	    activity_score: number | null;
20	    readiness_score: number | null;
21	    steps: number | null;
22	    total_calories: number | null;
23	  }>();
24	
25	  if (!results.length) return null;
26	
27	  const row = results[0];
28	  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });
29	  const { text: insight } = await generateText({
30	    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
31	    system: INSIGHT_PROMPT,
32	    prompt: `日付: ${row.day}\n睡眠スコア: ${row.sleep_score}\n活動スコア: ${row.activity_score}\nコンディション: ${row.readiness_score}\n歩数: ${row.steps}\n消費カロリー: ${row.total_calories}`,
33	  });
34	
35	  await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, insight, "insight", {
36	    day: row.day,
37	    source: "daily_auto",
38	  });
39	
40	  return insight;
41	}
42	
43	export async function generateWeeklySummary(env: Env): Promise<string | null> {
44	  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
45	    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score, steps, total_calories
46	     FROM v_oura_daily_summary
47	     WHERE day >= date('now', '-7 days')
48	     ORDER BY day`
49	  ).all<{
50	    day: string;
51	    sleep_score: number | null;
52	    activity_score: number | null;
53	    readiness_score: number | null;
54	    steps: number | null;
55	    total_calories: number | null;
56	  }>();
57	
58	  if (!results.length) return null;
59	
60	  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });
61	  const { text: summary } = await generateText({
62	    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
63	    system: `You are a health data analyst. Given a week of health metrics, write a comprehensive weekly summary in Japanese (4-6 sentences). Include averages, best/worst days, and trends.`,
64	    prompt: `週間データ:\n${JSON.stringify(results, null, 2)}`,
65	  });
66	
67	  const now = new Date();
68	  const weekStart = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString().split("T")[0];
69	  const weekEnd = new Date(now.getTime() - 86400000).toISOString().split("T")[0];
70	
71	  await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, summary, "weekly_summary", {
72	    period_start: weekStart,
73	    period_end: weekEnd,
74	    source: "weekly_auto",
75	  });
76	
77	  return summary;
78	}
79	
80	export async function retrieveRelevantContext(
81	  env: Env,
82	  query: string,
83	  topK = 3
84	): Promise<string | null> {
85	  const results = await semanticSearch(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, query, topK);
86	
87	  if (!results.length) return null;
88	
89	  return results.map((r) => `[${r.contentType}] ${r.content}`).join("\n\n");
90	}

1	import { generateText } from "ai";
2	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
3	import { embedAndStore } from "./embeddings";
4	import type { Env } from "./types";
5	
6	const MODEL_ID = "@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast" as const;
7	
8	const REPORT_PROMPT = `You are a health data analyst. Generate a detailed health report in Japanese Markdown format.
9	Include:
10	- 概要 (1-2 sentences summary)
11	- 睡眠分析 (scores, trends, best/worst days)
12	- 活動分析 (steps, calories, activity scores)
13	- コンディション分析 (readiness scores, temperature)
14	- まとめと改善提案
15	
16	Use headers (##), bullet points, and bold for emphasis.`;
17	
18	export type ReportResult = {
19	  id: string;
20	  title: string;
21	  r2Key: string;
22	  summary: string;
23	};
24	
25	export async function generateHealthReport(
26	  env: Env,
27	  type: string,
28	  periodStart: string,
29	  periodEnd: string
30	): Promise<ReportResult> {
31	  const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(
32	    `SELECT day, sleep_score, activity_score, readiness_score,
33	            steps, total_calories, active_calories, temperature_deviation,
34	            sleep_deep_sleep, sleep_efficiency, sleep_total_sleep
35	     FROM v_oura_daily_summary
36	     WHERE day >= ? AND day <= ?
37	     ORDER BY day`
38	  )
39	    .bind(periodStart, periodEnd)
40	    .all();
41	
42	  if (!results.length) {
43	    throw new Error(`期間 ${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd} のデータが見つかりません。`);
44	  }
45	
46	  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });
47	
48	  // Generate full report
49	  const { text: reportContent } = await generateText({
50	    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
51	    system: REPORT_PROMPT,
52	    prompt: `期間: ${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd}\nデータ件数: ${results.length}日分\n\nデータ:\n${JSON.stringify(results, null, 2)}`,
53	  });
54	
55	  // Generate concise summary for embedding
56	  const { text: summary } = await generateText({
57	    model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
58	    system: "Summarize the following health report in 2-3 sentences in Japanese.",
59	    prompt: reportContent,
60	  });
61	
62	  const id = crypto.randomUUID();
63	  const r2Key = `reports/${type}/${periodStart}_${periodEnd}.md`;
64	  const title = `${type === "weekly_health" ? "週次" : type === "monthly_health" ? "月次" : "カスタム"}ヘルスレポート (${periodStart} ~ ${periodEnd})`;
65	
66	  // Store full report in R2
67	  await env.DATA_LAKE.put(r2Key, reportContent, {
68	    customMetadata: { report_id: id, type, period_start: periodStart, period_end: periodEnd },
69	  });
70	
71	  // Embed summary in Vectorize
72	  const vectorId = await embedAndStore(env.AI, env.VECTORIZE, env.DB, summary, "report", {
73	    report_id: id,
74	    period_start: periodStart,
75	    period_end: periodEnd,
76	  });
77	
78	  // Store metadata in D1
79	  await env.DB.prepare(
80	    `INSERT INTO agent_reports (id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, r2_key, summary, vector_id)
81	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`
82	  )
83	    .bind(id, type, title, periodStart, periodEnd, r2Key, summary, vectorId)
84	    .run();
85	
86	  return { id, title, r2Key, summary };
87	}
88	
89	export async function listReports(
90	  db: D1Database,
91	  type?: string,
92	  limit = 10
93	): Promise<{ results: unknown[] }> {
94	  const query = type
95	    ? "SELECT id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, summary, created_at FROM agent_reports WHERE report_type = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?"
96	    : "SELECT id, report_type, title, period_start, period_end, summary, created_at FROM agent_reports ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?";
97	
98	  const stmt = type ? db.prepare(query).bind(type, limit) : db.prepare(query).bind(limit);
99	  const result = await stmt.all();
100	  return { results: result.results };
101	}
102

1	import { generateText } from "ai";
2	import { createWorkersAI } from "workers-ai-provider";
3	import { retrieveRelevantContext } from "./knowledge";
4	import { D1_SCHEMA } from "./schema";
5	import type { Env } from "./types";
6	import { isSafeQuery } from "./utils";
7	
8	const MODEL_ID = "@cf/meta/llama-3.3-70b-instruct-fp8-fast" as const;
9	
10	const SQL_GEN_PROMPT = `You are a SQL generator for a SQLite (D1) database containing Oura Ring health data.
11	Given a user question, output ONLY a valid SELECT SQL query. No explanation, no markdown, no backticks. Just the raw SQL.
12	
13	Database schema:
14	${D1_SCHEMA}
15	
16	SQL hints:
17	- "yesterday" = date('now', '-1 day')
18	- "this week" = day >= date('now', '-7 days')
19	- "this month" = strftime('%Y-%m', day) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')
20	- Prefer v_oura_daily_summary view
21	- Always add LIMIT 30
22	- Date columns are TEXT (YYYY-MM-DD format)
23	
24	Output ONLY the SQL query. Nothing else.`;
25	
26	const SUMMARIZE_PROMPT = `You are a health data assistant. Given the user's question and query results from an Oura Ring database, provide a concise answer in Japanese.
27	If no data is returned, say "データが見つかりませんでした。"
28	Keep the answer short and helpful.`;
29	
30	type SlackEvent =
31	  | { type: "url_verification"; challenge: string }
32	  | {
33	      type: "event_callback";
34	      event: {
35	        type: string;
36	        text?: string;
37	        user?: string;
38	        channel?: string;
39	        bot_id?: string;
40	        ts?: string;
41	        thread_ts?: string;
42	      };
43	    };
44	
45	async function verifySlackSignature(
46	  request: Request,
47	  body: string,
48	  signingSecret: string
49	): Promise<boolean> {
50	  const timestamp = request.headers.get("x-slack-request-timestamp");
51	  const signature = request.headers.get("x-slack-signature");
52	  if (!timestamp || !signature) return false;
53	
54	  // Reject requests older than 5 minutes
55	  if (Math.abs(Date.now() / 1000 - Number(timestamp)) > 300) return false;
56	
57	  const baseString = `v0:${timestamp}:${body}`;
58	  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
59	    "raw",
60	    new TextEncoder().encode(signingSecret),
61	    { name: "HMAC", hash: "SHA-256" },
62	    false,
63	    ["sign"]
64	  );
65	  const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign("HMAC", key, new TextEncoder().encode(baseString));
66	  const hex = [...new Uint8Array(sig)].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
67	  return signature === `v0=${hex}`;
68	}
69	
70	async function postSlackMessage(token: string, channel: string, text: string, threadTs?: string) {
71	  const response = await fetch("https://slack.com/api/chat.postMessage", {
72	    method: "POST",
73	    headers: {
74	      Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
75	      "Content-Type": "application/json",
76	    },
77	    body: JSON.stringify({
78	      channel,
79	      text,
80	      ...(threadTs ? { thread_ts: threadTs } : {}),
81	    }),
82	  });
83	
84	  if (!response.ok) {
85	    console.error("Slack API HTTP error:", response.status, response.statusText);
86	  } else {
87	    const result = (await response.json()) as { ok: boolean; error?: string };
88	    if (!result.ok) {
89	      console.error("Slack API error:", result.error);
90	    }
91	  }
92	}
93	
94	function extractSql(raw: string): string {
95	  // Strip markdown code blocks if present
96	  let sql = raw
97	    .replace(/```sql\n?/gi, "")
98	    .replace(/```\n?/g, "")
99	    .trim();
100	  // Take only first statement
101	  const semicolonIdx = sql.indexOf(";");
102	  if (semicolonIdx > 0) {
103	    sql = sql.substring(0, semicolonIdx + 1);
104	  }
105	  return sql;
106	}
107	
108	export async function handleSlackEvent(request: Request, env: Env): Promise<Response> {
109	  const body = await request.text();
110	
111	  if (!(await verifySlackSignature(request, body, env.SLACK_SIGNING_SECRET))) {
112	    return new Response("Invalid signature", { status: 401 });
113	  }
114	
115	  const event: SlackEvent = JSON.parse(body);
116	
117	  // Slack URL verification challenge
118	  if (event.type === "url_verification") {
119	    return new Response(event.challenge, { headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain" } });
120	  }
121	
122	  if (event.type !== "event_callback") {
123	    return new Response("OK");
124	  }
125	
126	  const { event: slackEvent } = event;
127	
128	  // Ignore bot messages to avoid loops
129	  if (slackEvent.bot_id || slackEvent.type !== "app_mention") {
130	    return new Response("OK");
131	  }
132	
133	  const userText = slackEvent.text?.replace(/<@[A-Z0-9]+>/g, "").trim();
134	  if (!userText || !slackEvent.channel) {
135	    return new Response("OK");
136	  }
137	
138	  const channel = slackEvent.channel;
139	  const threadTs = slackEvent.thread_ts ?? slackEvent.ts;
140	
141	  const promise = (async () => {
142	    try {
143	      const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: env.AI });
144	
145	      // Step 1: Generate SQL from user question
146	      const { text: rawSql } = await generateText({
147	        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
148	        system: SQL_GEN_PROMPT,
149	        prompt: userText,
150	      });
151	
152	      const sql = extractSql(rawSql);
153	
154	      if (!isSafeQuery(sql)) {
155	        await postSlackMessage(
156	          env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
157	          channel,
158	          "SELECT クエリのみ実行可能です。",
159	          threadTs
160	        );
161	        return;
162	      }
163	
164	      // Step 2: Execute SQL
165	      const queryResult = await env.DB.prepare(sql).all();
166	      const rows = queryResult.results.slice(0, 50);
167	
168	      // Step 2.5: Retrieve relevant context from knowledge base for RAG
169	      const ragResults = await retrieveRelevantContext(env, userText, 2);
170	      const contextBlock = ragResults ? `\n\n関連する過去のインサイト:\n${ragResults}` : "";
171	
172	      // Step 3: Summarize results with RAG context (truncate to avoid exceeding model context limits)
173	      const rowsPreview =
174	        rows.length > 10 ? [...rows.slice(0, 10), `... and ${rows.length - 10} more rows`] : rows;
175	      const { text: answer } = await generateText({
176	        model: workersai(MODEL_ID),
177	        system: SUMMARIZE_PROMPT + contextBlock,
178	        prompt: `ユーザーの質問: ${userText}\n\n実行したSQL: ${sql}\n\nクエリ結果 (${queryResult.results.length}件):\n${JSON.stringify(rowsPreview, null, 2)}`,
179	      });
180	
181	      const message = `${answer}\n\n\`\`\`${sql}\`\`\``;
182	      await postSlackMessage(env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN, channel, message, threadTs);
183	    } catch (error) {
184	      console.error("Slack handler error:", error);
185	      await postSlackMessage(
186	        env.SLACK_BOT_TOKEN,
187	        channel,
188	        `エラーが発生しました: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
189	        threadTs
190	      );
191	    }
192	  })();
193	
194	  globalThis.__SLACK_WAIT_UNTIL?.(promise);
195	
196	  return new Response("OK");
197	}
198	
```

> AGENT

把握しました。方針:

1. **`models.ts` 新規作成**: モデル定義 + `generateTextWithFallback` / `streamTextWithFallback` ヘルパー
2. **チャットモデル**: `qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8`($0.051/M、最安クラス）→ フォールバック `llama-3-8b-instruct-awq`（$0.123/M）
3. **埋め込みモデル**: `bge-small-en-v1.5`（$0.020/M、384次元）に変更 → Vectorize インデックスも384次元に
4. 全ファイルのモデル直指定を `models.ts` 経由に統一

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/models.ts",
  "content": "import {\n  type GenerateTextResult,\n  type StreamTextResult,\n  generateText,\n  streamText,\n} from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\n\n/**\n * チャットモデル: Neuron 消費が少ない順にフォールバック\n * - qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8:  $0.051/M input, $0.335/M output (最安 30B クラス)\n * - llama-3-8b-instruct-awq: $0.123/M input, $0.266/M output (8B フォールバック)\n */\nexport const CHAT_MODELS = [\n  \"@cf/qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8\",\n  \"@cf/meta/llama-3-8b-instruct-awq\",\n] as const;\n\n/**\n * 埋め込みモデル: 384次元、$0.020/M input (最安クラス)\n */\nexport const EMBEDDING_MODEL = \"@cf/baai/bge-small-en-v1.5\" as const;\nexport const EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS = 384;\n\n/**\n * generateText をモデルフォールバック付きで実行する。\n * CHAT_MODELS を順に試し、最初に成功したものを返す。\n */\nexport async function generateTextWithFallback(\n  ai: Ai,\n  options: Omit<Parameters<typeof generateText>[0], \"model\">\n): Promise<GenerateTextResult<Record<string, never>, never>> {\n  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: ai });\n  let lastError: unknown;\n\n  for (const modelId of CHAT_MODELS) {\n    try {\n      return await generateText({\n        ...options,\n        model: workersai(modelId),\n      });\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(`Model ${modelId} failed, trying next:`, error);\n      lastError = error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  throw lastError ?? new Error(\"All chat models failed\");\n}\n\n/**\n * streamText をモデルフォールバック付きで実行する。\n * CHAT_MODELS を順に試し、最初に成功したものを返す。\n */\nexport async function streamTextWithFallback(\n  ai: Ai,\n  options: Omit<Parameters<typeof streamText>[0], \"model\">\n): Promise<StreamTextResult<Record<string, never>, never>> {\n  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: ai });\n  let lastError: unknown;\n\n  for (const modelId of CHAT_MODELS) {\n    try {\n      const result = streamText({\n        ...options,\n        model: workersai(modelId),\n      });\n      // Await the first response to verify the model is available\n      await result.usage;\n      return result;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(`Model ${modelId} failed, trying next:`, error);\n      lastError = error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  throw lastError ?? new Error(\"All chat models failed\");\n}\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/agent.ts",
  "content": "import { AIChatAgent } from \"@cloudflare/ai-chat\";\nimport { convertToModelMessages, stepCountIs, streamText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\nimport { retrieveRelevantContext } from \"./knowledge\";\nimport { CHAT_MODELS } from \"./models\";\nimport { createTools, SYSTEM_PROMPT } from \"./tools\";\nimport type { Env } from \"./types\";\n\nexport class DataAgent extends AIChatAgent<Env> {\n  async onChatMessage(\n    _onFinish: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[0],\n    options?: Parameters<AIChatAgent<Env>[\"onChatMessage\"]>[1]\n  ) {\n    const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: this.env.AI });\n\n    // RAG: retrieve relevant context for the latest user message\n    let ragContext = \"\";\n    const lastUserMsg = this.messages.filter((m) => m.role === \"user\").at(-1);\n    if (lastUserMsg?.parts) {\n      const queryText = lastUserMsg.parts\n        .filter((p): p is { type: \"text\"; text: string } => p.type === \"text\")\n        .map((p) => p.text)\n        .join(\" \");\n\n      if (queryText) {\n        const context = await retrieveRelevantContext(this.env, queryText, 3);\n        if (context) {\n          ragContext = `\\n\\n関連する過去のインサイト:\\n${context}`;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    const streamOptions = {\n      system: SYSTEM_PROMPT + ragContext,\n      messages: await convertToModelMessages(this.messages),\n      abortSignal: options?.abortSignal,\n      stopWhen: stepCountIs(5),\n      tools: createTools(this.env),\n    };\n\n    // Model fallback: try each model in order\n    let lastError: unknown;\n    for (const modelId of CHAT_MODELS) {\n      try {\n        const result = streamText({\n          ...streamOptions,\n          model: workersai(modelId),\n        });\n        return result.toUIMessageStreamResponse();\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(`Model ${modelId} failed, trying next:`, error);\n        lastError = error;\n      }\n    }\n\n    throw lastError ?? new Error(\"All chat models failed\");\n  }\n}\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/takumi.abe/Developer/github.com/ta93abe/data-engineering-with-cloudflare/agents/src/models.ts",
  "content": "import { generateText } from \"ai\";\nimport { createWorkersAI } from \"workers-ai-provider\";\n\n/**\n * チャットモデル: Neuron 消費が少ない順にフォールバック\n * - qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8:  $0.051/M input, $0.335/M output (最安 30B クラス)\n * - llama-3-8b-instruct-awq: $0.123/M input, $0.266/M output (8B フォールバック)\n */\nexport const CHAT_MODELS = [\n  \"@cf/qwen/qwen3-30b-a3b-fp8\",\n  \"@cf/meta/llama-3-8b-instruct-awq\",\n] as const;\n\n/**\n * 埋め込みモデル: 384次元、$0.020/M input (最安クラス)\n */\nexport const EMBEDDING_MODEL = \"@cf/baai/bge-small-en-v1.5\" as const;\nexport const EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS = 384;\n\n/**\n * generateText をモデルフォールバック付きで実行する。\n * CHAT_MODELS を順に試し、最初に成功したものを返す。\n */\nexport async function generateTextWithFallback(\n  ai: Ai,\n  options: { system?: string; prompt: string }\n): Promise<{ text: string }> {\n  const workersai = createWorkersAI({ binding: ai });\n  let lastError: unknown;\n\n  for (const modelId of CHAT_MODELS) {\n    try {\n      const result = await generateText({\n        model: workersai(modelId),\n        system: options.system,\n        prompt: options.prompt,\n      });\n      return { text: result.text };\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error(`Model ${modelId} failed, trying next:`, error);\n      lastError = error;\n    }\n  }\n\n  throw lastError ?? new Error(\"All chat models failed\");\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

PR に push しました。変更点まとめ:

### モデル構成 (Before → After)

| 用途 | Before | After | コスト削減 |
|------|--------|-------|-----------|
| チャット/分析 | Llama 3.3 **70B** | Qwen3 **30B** a3b-fp8 | ~90%減 ($0.051/M) |
| フォールバック | なし | Llama 3 **8B** awq | 最安の安全ネット |
| 埋め込み | bge-**base** (768d, $0.067/M) | bge-**small** (384d, $0.020/M) | ~70%減 |

### フォールバックの仕組み

`models.ts` に `generateTextWithFallback(ai, options)` を集約。全ファイル（slack, knowledge, reports）が共通関数を使用:

```
Qwen3 30B → 失敗 → Llama 3 8B → 失敗 → throw
```

`agent.ts`（ストリーミング）は `streamText` を直接フォールバックループで実行。

### デプロイ時の注意

Vectorize インデックス作成コマンドが変更:
```bash
wrangler vectorize create data-agent-index --dimensions=384 --metric=cosine
```
（768 → **384** 次元）

### Usage examples

The following table defines a number of example use-cases and the estimated monthly cost for querying a Vectorize index. These estimates do not include the Vectorize usage that is part of the Workers Free and Paid plans.

| Workload   | Dimensions per vector | Stored dimensions | Queries per month | Calculation                                                                 | Estimated total     |
| ---------- | --------------------- | ----------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
| Experiment | 384                   | 5,000 vectors     | 10,000            | ((10000+5000)\*384\*(0.01/1000000)) + (5000\*384\*(0.05/100000000))         | $0.06 / mo included |
| Scaling    | 768                   | 25,000 vectors    | 50,000            | ((50000+25000)\*768\*(0.01/1000000)) + (25000\*768\*(0.05/100000000))       | $0.59 / mo most     |
| Production | 768                   | 50,000 vectors    | 200,000           | ((200000+50000)\*768\*(0.01/1000000)) + (50000\*768\*(0.05/100000000))      | $1.94 / mo          |
| Large      | 768                   | 250,000 vectors   | 500,000           | ((500000+250000)\*768\*(0.01/1000000)) + (250000\*768\*(0.05/100000000))    | $5.86 / mo          |
| XL         | 1536                  | 500,000 vectors   | 1,000,000         | ((1000000+500000)\*1536\*(0.01/1000000)) + (500000\*1536\*(0.05/100000000)) | $23.42 / mo         |

included All of this usage would fall into the Vectorize usage included in the Workers Free or Paid plan.

most Most of this usage would fall into the Vectorize usage included within the Workers Paid plan.

## Frequently Asked Questions

Frequently asked questions related to Vectorize pricing:

* Will Vectorize always have a free tier?

Yes, the [Workers free tier](/workers/platform/pricing/#workers) will always include the ability to prototype and experiment with Vectorize for free.

<title></title>
<text>
# Pricing

AI Gateway is available to use on all plans.

AI Gateway's core features available today are offered for free, and all it takes is a Cloudflare account and one line of code to [get started](/ai-gateway/get-started/). Core features include: dashboard analytics, caching, and rate limiting.

We will continue to build and expand AI Gateway. Some new features may be additional core features that will be free while others may be part of a premium plan. We will announce these as they become available.

You can monitor your usage in the AI Gateway dashboard.

## Persistent logs

Persistent logs are available on all plans, with a free allocation for both free and paid plans. Charges for additional logs beyond those limits are based on the number of logs stored per month.

### Free allocation and overage pricing

| Plan         | Free logs stored     | Overage pricing               |
| ------------ | -------------------- | ----------------------------- |
| Workers Free | 100,000 logs total   | N/A - Upgrade to Workers Paid |
| Workers Paid | 1,000,000 logs total | N/A                           |

Allocations are based on the total logs stored across all gateways. For guidance on managing or deleting logs, please see our [documentation](/ai-gateway/observability/logging).

## Logpush

Logpush is only available on the Workers Paid plan.

| Paid plan |                                    |
| --------- | ---------------------------------- |
| Requests  | 10 million / month, +$0.05/million |

## Fine print

Prices subject to change. If you are an Enterprise customer, reach out to your account team to confirm pricing details.


</text>
</result>

Workers AI has updated pricing to be more granular, with per-model unit-based pricing presented, but still billing in neurons in the back end.

<title></title>
<text>
## R2 pricing

| Standard storage                   | Infrequent Access storage    |                              |
| ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| Storage                            | $0.015 / GB-month            | $0.01 / GB-month             |
| Class A Operations                 | $4.50 / million requests     | $9.00 / million requests     |
| Class B Operations                 | $0.36 / million requests     | $0.90 / million requests     |
| Data Retrieval (processing)        | None                         | $0.01 / GB                   |
| Egress (data transfer to Internet) | Free [1](#user-content-fn-1) | Free [1](#user-content-fn-1) |

Billable unit rounding

Cloudflare rounds up your usage to the next billing unit.

For example:

* If you have performed one million and one operations, you will be billed for two million operations.
* If you have used 1.1 GB-month, you will be billed for 2 GB-month.
* If you have retrieved data (for infrequent access storage) for 1.1 GB, you will be billed for 2 GB.

### Free tier

You can use the following amount of storage and operations each month for free.

| Free                               |                              |
| ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| Storage                            | 10 GB-month / month          |
| Class A Operations                 | 1 million requests / month   |
| Class B Operations                 | 10 million requests / month  |
| Egress (data transfer to Internet) | Free [1](#user-content-fn-1) |

Warning

The free tier only applies to Standard storage, and does not apply to Infrequent Access storage.

### Storage usage

Storage is billed using gigabyte-month (GB-month) as the billing metric. A GB-month is calculated by averaging the _peak_ storage per day over a billing period (30 days).

For example:

Copy page

# Pricing

R2 charges based on the total volume of data stored, along with two classes of operations on that data:

1. [Class A operations](#class-a-operations) which are more expensive and tend to mutate state.
2. [Class B operations](#class-b-operations) which tend to read existing state.

For the Infrequent Access storage class, [data retrieval](#data-retrieval) fees apply. There are no charges for egress bandwidth for any storage class.

All included usage is on a monthly basis.

Note

To learn about potential cost savings from using R2, refer to the [R2 pricing calculator ↗](https://r2-calculator.cloudflare.com/).

## R2 pricing


## Cloudflare billing policy

To learn more about how usage is billed, refer to [Cloudflare Billing Policy](/billing/billing-policy/).

## Frequently asked questions

### Will I be charged for unauthorized requests to my R2 bucket?

No. You are not charged for operations when the caller does not have permission to make the request (HTTP 401 `Unauthorized` response status code).

## Footnotes

1. Egressing directly from R2, including via the [Workers API](/r2/api/workers/), [S3 API](/r2/api/s3/), and [r2.dev domains](/r2/buckets/public-buckets/#enable-managed-public-access) does not incur data transfer (egress) charges and is free. If you connect other metered services to an R2 bucket, you may be charged by those services. [↩](#user-content-fnref-1) [↩2](#user-content-fnref-1-2) [↩3](#user-content-fnref-1-3)

<title></title>
<text>
## R2

R2 charges based on the total volume of data stored, along with two classes of operations on that data:

1. **Class A operations** which are more expensive and tend to mutate state.
2. **Class B operations** which tend to read existing state.

There are no charges for egress bandwidth.

| Free                               | Standard storage            | Infrequent Access storage |                          |
| ---------------------------------- | --------------------------- | ------------------------- | ------------------------ |
| Storage                            | 10 GB-month / month         | $0.015 / GB-month         | $0.01 / GB-month         |
| Class A Operations                 | 1 million requests / month  | $4.50 / million requests  | $9.00 / million requests |
| Class B Operations                 | 10 million requests / month | $0.36 / million requests  | $0.90 / million requests |
| Data Retrieval (processing)        | None                        | None                      | $0.01 / GB               |
| Egress (data transfer to Internet) | Free                        | Free                      | Free                     |

R2 documentation

To learn more about R2 pricing, including billing examples, refer to [R2 Pricing](/r2/pricing/).

## Containers

Containers are billed for every 10ms that they are actively running at the following rates, with included monthly usage as part of the $5 USD per month [Workers Paid plan](/workers/platform/pricing/):

# Getting started with R2

* [  Watch this episode ](#tab-panel-5081)

# Getting started with R2

* [  Watch this episode ](#tab-panel-5081)
* [  Series overview ](#tab-panel-5082)

Cloudflare R2 is an object storage solution designed to handle your data and files efficiently. It is ideal for various use cases, such as storing large media files, creating data lakes, or delivering web assets. R2 offers a flexible architecture to suit your needs, all with zero egress fees, meaning you don't have to worry about unexpected costs when moving your data.

**Related content**

If you want to dive into detail, refer to the following pages:

* [Create new buckets](/r2/buckets/create-buckets/)
* [Bucket-level operations](/r2/buckets/create-buckets/#bucket-level-operations)

[ Watch Episode 1: Getting started with R2 ](/learning-paths/r2-intro/series/r2-1/) In this video, you'll learn how to use Cloudflare R2 to store and manage data with no egress fees, supporting use cases like media, web assets, backups, and static site hosting. 

[ Watch Episode 2: Public buckets ](/learning-paths/r2-intro/series/r2-2/) This video outlines how to configure public buckets in R2, which are private by default, to enable public Internet access. It presents two methods for setting up public access: using a custom domain or a Cloudflare managed subdomain. 

[ Watch Episode 3: S3 API compatibility ](/learning-paths/r2-intro/series/r2-3/) In this video, you'll learn how to use S3 API commands and tools with R2, set up and configure R2 for S3 API compatibility, and integrate R2 into your existing workflows with minimal changes. 

[ Watch Episode 4: Migrating your bucket data ](/learning-paths/r2-intro/series/r2-4/) In this video, you will learn how to use Cloudflare R2's data migration tools to transfer data from other cloud providers such as Amazon S3 and Google Cloud Storage to R2.

# How R2 works

Cloudflare R2 is an S3-compatible object storage service with no egress fees, built on Cloudflare's global network. It is [strongly consistent](/r2/reference/consistency/) and designed for high [data durability](/r2/reference/durability/).

R2 is ideal for storing and serving unstructured data that needs to be accessed frequently over the internet, without incurring egress fees. It's a good fit for workloads like serving web assets, training AI models, and managing user-generated content.

## Architecture

R2's architecture is composed of multiple components:

<title></title>
<text>
# Egress-free object storage in multi-cloud setups

**Last reviewed:**  almost 2 years ago 

## Introduction

Object storage is a modern data storage approach that stores data as objects rather than in a hierarchical structure like traditional file systems, making object storage highly scalable and flexible for managing vast amounts of data across diverse applications and environments.

Oftentimes organizations leverage multiple cloud providers to distribute their workloads across different platforms, mitigating risks associated with vendor lock-in, enhancing resilience, and optimizing performance and cost. However, managing data across multiple clouds introduces challenges related to data mobility and interoperability, particularly when it comes to transferring data between cloud providers or on-premises environments.

Egress fees are charges incurred when data is transferred out of a cloud provider's network, either to another cloud provider, on-premises infrastructure, or external services. These fees can vary depending on factors such as the volume of data transferred, the destination of the data, and the network bandwidth utilized.

[R2](/r2/) offers an enticing value proposition by not charging the costly egress bandwidth fees associated with typical cloud storage services. This can be very advantageous in the context of multi-cloud environments, especially when you want to run compute-intensive workloads such as AI model training, query engines, and other data science tools.

## R2 multi-cloud setup

![Figure 1: R2 multi-cloud setup](/_astro/r2-multi-cloud.jB-KW29c_Z1XXhic.svg "Figure 1: R2-multi-cloud setup")

Figure 1: R2-multi-cloud setup

## 2025-01-13

**D1 will begin enforcing its free tier limits from the 10th of February 2025.**

D1 will begin enforcing the daily [free tier limits](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/platform/limits) from 2025-02-10\. These limits only apply to accounts on the Workers Free plan.

From 2025-02-10, if you do not take any action and exceed the daily free tier limits, queries to D1 databases via the Workers API and/or REST API will return errors until limits reset daily at 00:00 UTC.

To ensure uninterrupted service, upgrade your account to the [Workers Paid plan](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/) from the [plans page](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?account=/workers/plans). The minimum monthly billing amount is $5\. Refer to [Workers Paid plan](https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/pricing/) and [D1 limits](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/platform/limits/).

For better insight into your current usage, refer to your [billing metrics](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/observability/billing/) for rows read and rows written, which can be found on the [D1 dashboard](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?account=/workers/d1) or GraphQL API.

## 2025-01-07

**D1 Worker API request latency decreases by 40-60%.**

D1 lowered end-to-end Worker API request latency by 40-60% by eliminating redundant network round trips for each request.

![D1 Worker API latency](https://developers.cloudflare.com/images/d1/faster-d1-worker-api.png)


## 2025-02-19

**D1 supports \`PRAGMA optimize\`**

D1 now supports `PRAGMA optimize` command, which can improve database query performance. It is recommended to run this command after a schema change (for example, after creating an index). Refer to [PRAGMA optimize](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/sql-api/sql-statements/#pragma-optimize) for more information.

## 2025-02-04

**Fixed bug with D1 read-only access via UI and /query REST API.**

Fixed a bug with D1 permissions which allowed users with read-only roles via the UI and users with read-only API tokens via the `/query` [REST API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/api/resources/d1/subresources/database/methods/query/) to execute queries that modified databases. UI actions via the `Tables` tab, such as creating and deleting tables, were incorrectly allowed with read-only access. However, UI actions via the `Console` tab were not affected by this bug and correctly required write access.

Write queries with read-only access will now fail. If you relied on the previous incorrect behavior, please assign the correct roles to users or permissions to API tokens to perform D1 write queries.

## 2025-01-13

**D1 will begin enforcing its free tier limits from the 10th of February 2025.**

D1 will begin enforcing the daily [free tier limits](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/platform/limits) from 2025-02-10\. These limits only apply to accounts on the Workers Free plan.


## 2023-08-19

**Row count now returned per query**

D1 now returns a count of `rows_written` and `rows_read` for every query executed, allowing you to assess the cost of query for both [pricing](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/platform/pricing/) and [index optimization](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/best-practices/use-indexes/) purposes.

The `meta` object returned in [D1's Client API](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/worker-api/return-object/#d1result) contains a total count of the rows read (`rows_read`) and rows written (`rows_written`) by that query. For example, a query that performs a full table scan (for example, `SELECT * FROM users`) from a table with 5000 rows would return a `rows_read` value of `5000`:

```json
"meta": {
  "duration": 0.20472300052642825,
  "size_after": 45137920,
  "rows_read": 5000,
  "rows_written": 0
}

```

Refer to [D1 pricing documentation](https://developers.cloudflare.com/d1/platform/pricing/) to understand how reads and writes are measured. D1 remains free to use during the alpha period.

## 2023-08-09

**Bind D1 from the Cloudflare dashboard**

6. Indexes will add an additional written row when writes include the indexed column, as there are two rows written: one to the table itself, and one to the index. The performance benefit of an index and reduction in rows read will, in nearly all cases, offset this additional write.
7. Storage is based on gigabytes stored per month, and is based on the sum of all databases in your account. Tables and indexes both count towards storage consumed.

10. [Read replication](/d1/best-practices/read-replication/) does not charge extra for read replicas. You incur the same usage billing based on `rows_read` and `rows_written` by your queries.

## Frequently Asked Questions

Frequently asked questions related to D1 pricing:

### Will D1 always have a Free plan?

Yes, the [Workers Free plan](/workers/platform/pricing/#workers) will always include the ability to prototype and experiment with D1 for free.


## Frequently Asked Questions

Frequently asked questions related to D1 pricing:

### Will D1 always have a Free plan?

Yes, the [Workers Free plan](/workers/platform/pricing/#workers) will always include the ability to prototype and experiment with D1 for free.

### What happens if I exceed the daily limits on reads and writes, or the total storage limit, on the Free plan?

When your account hits the daily read and/or write limits, you will not be able to run queries against D1\. D1 API will return errors to your client indicating that your daily limits have been exceeded. Once you have reached your included storage limit, you will need to delete unused databases or clean up stale data before you can insert new data, create or alter tables or create indexes and triggers.

Upgrading to the Workers Paid plan will remove these limits, typically within minutes.

### What happens if I exceed the monthly included reads, writes and/or storage on the paid tier?

You will be billed for the additional reads, writes and storage according to [D1's pricing metrics](/d1/platform/pricing/#billing-metrics).

### How can I estimate my (eventual) bill?


### How can I estimate my (eventual) bill?

Every query returns a `meta` object that contains a total count of the rows read (`rows_read`) and rows written (`rows_written`) by that query. For example, a query that performs a full table scan (for instance, `SELECT * FROM users`) from a table with 5000 rows would return a `rows_read` value of `5000`:

```

"meta": {

  "duration": 0.20472300052642825,

  "size_after": 45137920,

  "rows_read": 5000,

  "rows_written": 0

}


```

These are also included in the D1 [Cloudflare dashboard ↗](https://dash.cloudflare.com) and the [analytics API](/d1/observability/metrics-analytics/), allowing you to attribute read and write volumes to specific databases, time periods, or both.

### Does D1 charge for data transfer / egress?

No.

### Does D1 charge additional for additional compute?

D1 itself does not charge for additional compute. Workers querying D1 and computing results: for example, serializing results into JSON and/or running queries, are billed per [Workers pricing](/workers/platform/pricing/#workers), in addition to your D1 specific usage.

### Do queries I run from the dashboard or Wrangler (the CLI) count as billable usage?

Yes, any queries you run against your database, including inserting (`INSERT`) existing data into a new database, table scans (`SELECT * FROM table`), or creating indexes count as either reads or writes.

### Can I use an index to reduce the number of rows read by a query?

# Pricing

Durable Objects can incur two types of billing: compute and storage.

Note

Durable Objects are available both on Workers Free and Workers Paid plans.

* **Workers Free plan**: Only Durable Objects with [SQLite storage backend](/durable-objects/reference/durable-objects-migrations/#create-migration) are available.
* **Workers Paid plan**: Durable Objects with either SQLite storage backend or [key-value storage backend](/durable-objects/reference/durable-objects-migrations/#create-durable-object-class-with-key-value-storage) are available.

If you wish to downgrade from a Workers Paid plan to a Workers Free plan, you must first ensure that you have deleted all Durable Object namespaces with the key-value storage backend.

On Workers Free plan:

* If you exceed any one of the free tier limits, further operations of that type will fail with an error.
* Daily free limits reset at 00:00 UTC.

## Compute billing


## Compute billing

Durable Objects are billed for compute duration (wall-clock time) while the Durable Object is actively running or is idle in memory but unable to [hibernate](/durable-objects/concepts/durable-object-lifecycle/). Durable Objects that are idle and eligible for hibernation are not billed for duration, even before the runtime has hibernated them. Requests to a Durable Object keep it active or create the object if it was inactive.

| Free plan | Paid plan         |                                                                                                                      |
| --------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requests  | 100,000 / day     | 1 million / month, + $0.15/million Includes HTTP requests, RPC sessions1, WebSocket messages2, and alarm invocations |
| Duration3 | 13,000 GB-s / day | 400,000 GB-s / month, + $12.50/million GB-s4,5                                                                       |

Footnotes

1 Each [RPC session](/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/) is billed as one request to your Durable Object. Every [RPC method call](/durable-objects/best-practices/create-durable-object-stubs-and-send-requests/) on a [Durable Objects stub](/durable-objects/) is its own RPC session and therefore a single billed request.

RPC method calls can return objects (stubs) extending [RpcTarget](/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/#lifetimes-memory-and-resource-management) and invoke calls on those stubs. Subsequent calls on the returned stub are part of the same RPC session and are not billed as separate requests. For example:

JavaScript

```

let durableObjectStub = OBJECT_NAMESPACE.get(id); // retrieve Durable Object stub

using foo = await durableObjectStub.bar(); // billed as a request

await foo.baz(); // treated as part of the same RPC session created by calling bar(), not billed as a request


## Compute billing examples

These examples exclude the costs for the Workers calling the Durable Objects. When modelling the costs of a Durable Object, note that:

* Inactive objects receiving no requests do not incur any duration charges.
* The [WebSocket Hibernation API](/durable-objects/best-practices/websockets/#durable-objects-hibernation-websocket-api) can dramatically reduce duration-related charges for Durable Objects communicating with clients over the WebSocket protocol, especially if messages are only transmitted occasionally at sparse intervals.

### Example 1

This example represents a simple Durable Object used as a co-ordination service invoked via HTTP.

* A single Durable Object was called by a Worker 1.5 million times
* It is active for 1,000,000 seconds in the month

In this scenario, the estimated monthly cost would be calculated as:

**Requests**:

* (1.5 million requests - included 1 million requests) x $0.15 / 1,000,000 = $0.075

**Compute Duration**:

* 1,000,000 seconds \* 128 MB / 1 GB = 128,000 GB-s
* (128,000 GB-s - included 400,000 GB-s) x $12.50 / 1,000,000 = $0.00

**Estimated total**: \~$0.075 (requests) + $0.00 (compute duration) + minimum $5/mo usage = $5.08 per month

### Example 2

This example represents a moderately trafficked Durable Objects based application using WebSockets to broadcast game, chat or real-time user state across connected clients:

### Compute billing

Durable Objects are billed for compute duration (wall-clock time) while the Durable Object is actively running or is idle in memory but unable to [hibernate](/durable-objects/concepts/durable-object-lifecycle/). Durable Objects that are idle and eligible for hibernation are not billed for duration, even before the runtime has hibernated them. Requests to a Durable Object keep it active or create the object if it was inactive.

| Free plan | Paid plan         |                                                                                                                      |
| --------- | ----------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requests  | 100,000 / day     | 1 million / month, + $0.15/million Includes HTTP requests, RPC sessions1, WebSocket messages2, and alarm invocations |
| Duration3 | 13,000 GB-s / day | 400,000 GB-s / month, + $12.50/million GB-s4,5                                                                       |

Footnotes

1 Each [RPC session](/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/) is billed as one request to your Durable Object. Every [RPC method call](/durable-objects/best-practices/create-durable-object-stubs-and-send-requests/) on a [Durable Objects stub](/durable-objects/) is its own RPC session and therefore a single billed request.

RPC method calls can return objects (stubs) extending [RpcTarget](/workers/runtime-apis/rpc/lifecycle/#lifetimes-memory-and-resource-management) and invoke calls on those stubs. Subsequent calls on the returned stub are part of the same RPC session and are not billed as separate requests. For example:

JavaScript

```

let durableObjectStub = OBJECT_NAMESPACE.get(id); // retrieve Durable Object stub

using foo = await durableObjectStub.bar(); // billed as a request

await foo.baz(); // treated as part of the same RPC session created by calling bar(), not billed as a request


#### Key-value storage backend

| Workers Paid plan     |                            |
| --------------------- | -------------------------- |
| Read request units1,2 | 1 million, + $0.20/million |
| Write request units3  | 1 million, + $1.00/million |
| Delete requests4      | 1 million, + $1.00/million |
| Stored data5          | 1 GB, + $0.20/ GB-month    |

Footnotes

1 A request unit is defined as 4 KB of data read or written. A request that writes or reads more than 4 KB will consume multiple units, for example, a 9 KB write will consume 3 write request units.

2 List operations are billed by read request units, based on the amount of data examined. For example, a list request that returns a combined 80 KB of keys and values will be billed 20 read request units. A list request that does not return anything is billed for 1 read request unit.

3 Each `setAlarm` is billed as a single write request unit.

4 Delete requests are unmetered. For example, deleting a 100 KB value will be charged one delete request.

5 Durable Objects will be billed for stored data until the data is removed. Once the data is removed, the object will be cleaned up automatically by the system.

Requests that hit the [Durable Objects in-memory cache](/durable-objects/reference/in-memory-state/) or that use the [multi-key versions of get()/put()/delete() methods](/durable-objects/api/sqlite-storage-api/) are billed the same as if they were a normal, individual request for each key.

Durable Objects billing examples

For more information and [examples of Durable Objects billing](/durable-objects/platform/pricing#compute-billing-examples), refer to [Durable Objects Pricing](/durable-objects/platform/pricing/).

## Vectorize

Vectorize is currently only available on the Workers paid plan.

return response;

  },

};


```

Free plan [limits](/durable-objects/platform/pricing/) apply to Durable Objects compute and storage usage. Limits allow developers to build real-world applications, with every Worker request able to call a Durable Object on the free plan.

For more information, checkout:

* [Documentation](/durable-objects/concepts/what-are-durable-objects/)
* [Zero-latency SQLite storage in every Durable Object blog ↗](https://blog.cloudflare.com/sqlite-in-durable-objects/)
## Durable Objects on Workers Free plan

Apr 07, 2025 

[ Durable Objects ](/durable-objects/)[ Workers ](/workers/) 

Durable Objects can now be used with zero commitment on the [Workers Free plan](/workers/platform/pricing/) allowing you to build AI agents with [Agents SDK](/agents/), collaboration tools, and real-time applications like chat or multiplayer games.

Durable Objects let you build stateful, serverless applications with millions of tiny coordination instances that run your application code alongside (in the same thread!) your durable storage. Each Durable Object can access its own SQLite database through a [Storage API](/durable-objects/best-practices/access-durable-objects-storage/). A Durable Object class is defined in a Worker script encapsulating the Durable Object's behavior when accessed from a Worker. To try the code below, click the button:

[![Deploy to Cloudflare](https://deploy.workers.cloudflare.com/button)](https://deploy.workers.cloudflare.com/?url=https://github.com/cloudflare/templates/tree/main/hello-world-do-template)

JavaScript

```

import { DurableObject } from "cloudflare:workers";


// Durable Object

export class MyDurableObject extends DurableObject {

  ...

  async sayHello(name) {

    return `Hello, ${name}!`;

  }

}


// Worker

export default {

  async fetch(request, env) {

    // Every unique ID refers to an individual instance of the Durable Object class

    const id = env.MY_DURABLE_OBJECT.idFromName("foo");


    // A stub is a client used to invoke methods on the Durable Object

    const stub = env.MY_DURABLE_OBJECT.get(id);


    // Methods on the Durable Object are invoked via the stub

    const response = await stub.sayHello("world");


    return response;

  },

};


```

Free plan [limits](/durable-objects/platform/pricing/) apply to Durable Objects compute and storage usage. Limits allow developers to build real-world applications, with every Worker request able to call a Durable Object on the free plan.

> AGENT

全サービスの料金を確認しました。結論: **個人利用レベルならほぼ無料で収まります。**

## 料金まとめ

| サービス | 無料枠 | このプロジェクトの想定使用量 | 料金見込み |
|---------|--------|------------------------|----------|
| **Workers AI** | 10,000 Neurons/日 | 数十回の会話/日 | **無料** (軽量モデルに変更済み) |
| **AI Gateway** | コア機能すべて無料 + 10万ログ (Free) | ログ/キャッシュ/レート制限 | **無料** |
| **Vectorize** | Free プランに含まれる | 384次元 × 数千ベクトル | **無料** ($0.06/月以下は含まれる) |
| **D1** | 500万 rows read/日, 10万 rows write/日, 5GB | エージェントクエリ + 日次書き込み | **無料** |
| **R2** | 10GB, 100万 Class A ops/月, 1000万 Class B ops/月, **エグレス無料** | Markdownレポート数十件 | **無料** |
| **Durable Objects** | 10万 requests/日, 13,000 GB-s/日 (SQLite backend, Free対応) | チャット/Slack 数十req/日 | **無料** |

## 注意点

**Workers Free プランでの制約:**
- Workers AI: **10,000 Neurons/日** が上限。超えたらその日は使えなくなる（翌日0:00 UTCリセット）
- D1: **500万 rows read/日**, **10万 rows write/日** が上限
- DO: **10万 requests/日** が上限

**Workers Paid ($5/月) に上げると:**
- Workers AI: 超過分 $0.011/1,000 Neurons で従量課金
- D1: 250億 rows read/月, 5000万 rows write/月 が含まれる
- DO: 100万 requests/月 が含まれる

### 現実的な見積もり

1日10〜20回の会話程度なら、**Free プランのままで全サービス無料枠内に収まる**と思います。Qwen3 30B + bge-small に変更したことで、1回の会話あたりの Neuron 消費が大幅に下がっているのが効いています。

# Astro

**Start from CLI**: Scaffold an Astro project on Workers, and pick your template.

 npm  yarn  pnpm 

```
npm create cloudflare@latest -- my-astro-app --framework=astro
```

```
yarn create cloudflare my-astro-app --framework=astro
```


## Deploy a new Astro project on Workers

1. **Create a new project with the create-cloudflare CLI (C3).**  
 npm  yarn  pnpm  
```  
npm create cloudflare@latest -- my-astro-app --framework=astro  
```  
```  
yarn create cloudflare my-astro-app --framework=astro  
```  
```  
pnpm create cloudflare@latest my-astro-app --framework=astro  
```  
What's happening behind the scenes?  
When you run this command, C3 creates a new project directory, initiates [Astro's official setup tool ↗](https://docs.astro.build/en/tutorial/1-setup/2/), and configures the project for Cloudflare. It then offers the option to instantly deploy your application to Cloudflare.
2. **Develop locally.**  
After creating your project, run the following command in your project directory to start a local development server.  
 npm  yarn  pnpm  
```  
npm run dev  
```  
```  
yarn run dev  
```  
```  
pnpm run dev  
```
3. **Deploy your project.**  
You can deploy your project to a [\*.workers.dev subdomain](/workers/configuration/routing/workers-dev/) or a [custom domain](/workers/configuration/routing/custom-domains/) from your local machine or any CI/CD system (including [Workers Builds](/workers/ci-cd/#workers-builds)). Use the following command to build and deploy. If you're using a CI service, be sure to update your "deploy command" accordingly.  
 npm  yarn  pnpm  
```  
npm run deploy  
```  
```  
yarn run deploy  
```  
```  
pnpm run deploy  
```

## Deploy an existing Astro project on Workers

Automatic configuration

Run `wrangler deploy` in a project without a Wrangler configuration file and Wrangler will automatically detect Astro, generate the necessary configuration, and deploy your project.

 npm  yarn  pnpm 

```
npx wrangler deploy
```


# Astro

**Start from CLI**: Scaffold an Astro project on Workers, and pick your template.

 npm  yarn  pnpm 

```
npm create cloudflare@latest -- my-astro-app --framework=astro
```

```
yarn create cloudflare my-astro-app --framework=astro
```

```
pnpm create cloudflare@latest my-astro-app --framework=astro
```

---

**Or just deploy**: Create a static blog with Astro and deploy it on Cloudflare Workers, with CI/CD and previews all set up for you.

[![Deploy to Workers](https://deploy.workers.cloudflare.com/button)](https://dash.cloudflare.com/?to=/:account/workers-and-pages/create/deploy-to-workers&repository=https://github.com/cloudflare/templates/tree/main/astro-blog-starter-template)

## What is Astro?

[Astro ↗](https://astro.build/) is a JavaScript web framework designed for creating websites that display large amounts of content (such as blogs, documentation sites, or online stores).

Astro emphasizes performance through minimal client-side JavaScript - by default, it renders as much content as possible at build time, or [on-demand ↗](https://docs.astro.build/en/guides/on-demand-rendering/) on the "server" - this can be a Cloudflare Worker. [“Islands” ↗](https://docs.astro.build/en/concepts/islands/) of JavaScript are added only where interactivity or personalization is needed.

Astro is also framework-agnostic, and supports every major UI framework, including React, Preact, Svelte, Vue, SolidJS, via its official [integrations ↗](https://astro.build/integrations/).
